DNp01 input 1,455 edges / 36,589 |W| with 0 matched and 0 sign changes; the five damped types 21 edges / 4,315 |W| (SAD073 1,177 GABA, DNp70 1,416 ACh, PVLP010 711 glutamate, CL367 537 GABA, GNG300 474 GABA), LC4 6,362 / 126 edges, LPLC2 4,862 / 185 edges. UNKNOWN_NT_OVERRIDE_REGEX relabels exactly 27 cells in the 13 listed types with the listed per-type counts, all from nt 'unknown' to 'gaba'; their outputs are 13,287 edges / 136,223 |W| = 0.1122 % of sum|W|, per cell min 380 / median 1,472 / max 26,314; target superclasses in |W| cb_intrinsic 106,111, cb_sensory 29,213, descending_neuron 858, ascending 16, visual_projection 13, '' 10, efferent_ascending 2; onto ORN-named types 26,097 |W|; 10,093 of the 13,287 edges (76,105 |W|) are receptor-matched with 0 sign changes. Recompiling with the regex emptied gives sum|W| 121,324,368 and 2,388 unknown-NT cells, identical to the cluster log, with the sparsity pattern bit-identical and only nt/sign (27 rows) and the derived in_syn columns differing; exactly 13,287 W entries change.
* KEY CLAIM 13 (the fixed break is real): at HEAD, flyverse/brain.py line 268 defines `_shaped_weights(c, p, receptor=None)` and line 350 calls `_shaped_weights(c, p, self.receptor)` positionally, while `git show HEAD:scripts/retire_measures.py` line 232 defines `def _protected(c, p)` and installs it unconditionally for every configuration -- so every retire_measures run at HEAD would raise TypeError. The fix (third positional argument threaded through _protected and all three hooks) is in the working-tree diff.
* KEY CLAIM 14 (tests): `python -m pytest tests/test_receptor_model.py tests/test_nt_readout.py tests/test_world.py tests/test_control.py -q` -> 64 passed in 28.80 s locally.
* No default was changed by this task. flyverse/optic.py is unmodified (DEFAULT_PAIR_GAIN LPi x4 intact) and the LPi ablation is an in-script rebuild; flyverse/connectome.py's diff contains no change to UNKNOWN_NT_OVERRIDE_REGEX or TYPE_NT_OVERRIDE_DEFAULT and the AL LN ablation is a try/finally monkeypatch around compile_connectome() with no save (the shared cache is never written). The only default change in the tree is the adoption task's brain.py `receptor_model: None -> 'sign'` / `receptor_net_rule: 'class' -> 'abs'`, and tests/test_receptor_model.py pins WEIGHTS_MD5_NONE so receptor_model=None still reproduces the pre-adoption weights byte for byte (test_none_reproduces_previous_weights_byte_for_byte, passing).
* The configurations do what they claim and nothing else: `python scripts/retire_measures.py --check --configs baseline,pair_gain_lpi_x1,no_gf_damping` (local, CPU, no simulation) shows pair_gain_lpi_x1 differing from baseline in exactly one pair_gain entry (LPi34|43 -> LPLC2 4.0 -> 1.0) and no_gf_damping dropping exactly the x0.3 type_path_gain row, both with lif.receptor_model 'sign' / 'abs'.
* The decision satisfies the criterion as written. anti_runaway.md's pre-existing rule ('a check counts as broken only when it fails in every replicate that ran the configuration'; the new section adds 'while passing in every baseline replicate') is met by all three ablations on walk.power_max_hz, and by no other check, in the actual JSONs.

## verify:exp:compass  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* FINDING (2), the 'narrower' half: refuted by the control the report never ran. I ran config `base` (silent GLNO, shared cache) on the SAME gD grid with the SAME seeds 0-2 (batch sk-glno-grid-9bf2b1, 6 jobs, 0 failed, 6.3 min, B200, torch 2.11.0+cu128; out/sk_base_gE1.75.json, out/sk_base_gE2.json, out/sk_base_gE2.5.json). Persist (n/3) silent vs gaba: 1.75/8 3 vs 3; 1.75/15 2 vs 0; 1.75/25 0 vs 0; 1.75/40 0 vs 0; 2/8 1 vs 3; 2/15 3 vs 3; 2/25 3 vs 2; 2/40 1 vs 1; 2.5/8 0 vs 2; 2.5/15 2 vs 2; 2.5/25 3 vs 3; 2.5/40 3 vs 2. Totals 21/36 persisting runs for EACH condition. The inhibitory GLNO MOVES the window down one gD step at gE 2 (3/3 at {15,25} silent -> {8,15} gaba) and 2.5, and GAINS points the silent ring cannot hold (2/8 1/3 -> 3/3; 2.5/8 0/3 -> 2/3, where silent seed 0-2 all sit in an undisplaceable 111-114 Hz bump); it is not narrower. The report compared its 3-seed gaba scan against a 1-seed table (cx_wedge.md section 6).
* The stated silent-GLNO reference window 'gE 1.75-2, gD 15-40, rho 3.75-13' is refuted at three of its four corners: with 3 seeds the silent ring is 0/3 at gE 1.75/gD 25 (in 11/8/7 Hz) and 0/3 at 1.75/40 (10/8/7), and 1/3 at gE 2/gD 40 (155/8/44); those points were never measured silent before (cx_wedge.md section 6 has no 1.75/25, 1.75/40 row) and rho 13 corresponds to 1.75/40, an unmeasured cell. Measured silent window = {1.75/8 3/3 (177-179 Hz, also never measured before), 2/15 3/3, 2/25 3/3, 2.5/25 3/3, 2.5/40 3/3}, rho 2.6-6.4.
* KEY CLAIM 3, the window point gE 1.75 / gD 8 '3/3 persist, 148-154 Hz': refuted as a window at fresh seeds. out/sk_gaba_s345.json (seeds 3,4,5, same config, own from-raw compile of cache_72164311): persist 0/3 - seed 3 in 17.2 Hz (4/11 above, PEN 2.4, GLNO 9.6), seed 4 in 9.9 Hz (2/11, PEN 0.8, GLNO 0.7), seed 5 in 154.5 Hz but out_above 4/35. Silent base at the identical seeds (out/sk_base_s345.json) holds 172.3 / 170.5 / 176.6 Hz with in_above 11/11/11. So over 6 seeds gE 1.75/gD 8 is 3/6 with two outright deaths, against base 5/6 (its one 'no' is the 4-cell rule). The derived statements 'the lowest persistent bump is 148-154 Hz at 1.75/8' and 'lowers the rate by ~25 % at the low-gain edge of its window' lose their basis; gE 2/gD 25 likewise drops to 3/6 (seed 3 jumps: in 46.5 / out 43.0, centre 4.1) against base 5/6.
* docs/audits/cx_glno.md section 4 Answers, 'GLNO itself fires at 96-162 Hz at 5 s inside the window': refuted. Over the 18 runs at the six window points GLNO at 5 s is 96.0-145.2 Hz (min 1.75/8 seed 1, max 2.5/25 seed 1). 162 is the 3-seed mean at gE 2.5 / gD 8 (170.2/158.6/156.1), a point the report itself scores 2/3 and excludes from the window.
* Same sentence, 'higher than any compass type but Delta7': refuted. GLNO exceeds Delta7 in all 18 window runs: 96.0-145.2 vs 82.7-108.4 Hz (e.g. gE 2/gD 15 seed 0: GLNO 114.8, Delta7 95.6; 1.75/8 seed 1: 96.0 vs 83.7). The only higher number in the row is the in-bump EPG mean (148-227 Hz), which is not a type mean.
* Section 4 caveat 'in this scan every no is a real jump, a stiff pre-pulse bump or a death, none is the 4-cell boundary case': true only for seeds 0-2. At seed 5 the boundary case fires at every point I ran, in BOTH conditions: out_mean 11.2 Hz gives out_above 4/35 while the bump is confined at 154-244 Hz with in_above 9-11 (out/sk_base_s345.json 1.75/8, 2/15, 2/25, 2.5/25 and out/sk_gaba_s345.json 2/15, 2/25, 2.5/25). The criterion's brittleness costs one seed in six systematically, so the 3/3 vs 2/3 distinctions the window rests on are within the criterion's own noise.
* Summary-table verdict 'gaba 1.75/15 ... dead (3/3 by 2 s)': refuted for seed 1, which holds the bump at 131 Hz (8/11 above) at 2 s and 134 Hz (8/11) at 3 s and collapses only between 3 and 5 s (12 Hz, 2/11). Seeds 0 and 2 are dead by 2 s and 1 s (out/cx_glno_gaba_gE1.75.json, t2.0_/t3.0_ fields).
* KEY CLAIM 5, 'wedge profile 133/234/159 Hz at wedges 3-5 at 5 s' for all three seed-1 jump rows: only gE 2/gD 25 has that profile. gE 2.25/40 seed 1 is 144/247/197 and gE 2.5/40 seed 1 is 182/270/238 (t5.0_wedge_profile). What is actually identical across the four rows (including round-2 base 1.75/15 seed 1, 133/231/160) is the jump LOCATION, centre 4.1, and the 13 non-bump wedges (10,5,13,...,11,3,14,17) - the seed-1 background realisation.
* KEY CLAIM 6, 'pre-pulse bump at wedges 12-14 (225-278 Hz)' and 'at gE >= 2.25 with gD <= 15': the per-wedge range is 206-287 Hz, not 225-278 (gE 2.5/gD 8: 283/287/285 at wedges 12-14; gE 2.25/gD 25: 206/231/226), and the pre-bump is present at seed 0 for every gE>=2.25 point including gD 25 and 40, not only gD <= 15 (at 2.25/40 it sits at wedges 1-3, i.e. on the driven tile). The capture / no-capture list itself (displaced at 2.25/15, 2.25/25, 2.5/25; not at 2.25/8, 2.5/8, 2.5/15) is correct.
* KEY CLAIM 9 job durations: the API (7 jobs, batch r3-glno-gaba-5f4869) gives started->finished 367.0, 367.3, 367.8, 403.3, 404.3, 471.3 s for the 12-run jobs and 113.9 s for the anchor, not '389-471 s' and '133 s' (doc: '6.5-7.9 min per job', 'the anchor job 2.2 min'; actual 6.1-7.9 min and 1.9 min). Submission time (06:44:14.8-06:44:15.9 UTC), all exit 0, node1, and the 06:52 finish are right.

### Corrections
* Replace 'the inhibitory-GLNO window is shifted down in gD by about 2x and narrower at low gE' with the seed-matched result: on the 12 points x 3 seeds I ran in both conditions the totals are equal (21/36 persisting runs each); the 3/3 window moves from silent {1.75/8, 2/15, 2/25, 2.5/25, 2.5/40} to gaba {1.75/8, 2/8, 2/15, 2.5/25}; gaba gains gE 2/gD 8 (1/3 -> 3/3) and gE 2.5/gD 8 (0/3 -> 2/3) and loses gE 1.75/gD 15 (2/3 -> 0/3), gE 2/gD 25 (3/3 -> 2/3) and gE 2.5/gD 40 (3/3 -> 2/3). Data: out/sk_base_gE{1.75,2,2.5}.json.
* Drop gE 1.75/gD 8 from the window list or mark it 3/6: seeds 3-5 give 17.2 / 9.9 / 154.5 Hz (out/sk_gaba_s345.json) where the silent ring gives 172.3 / 170.5 / 176.6. The '148-227 Hz' window range then starts at 180 Hz (gE 2/gD 15), and the rate answer becomes '-10 % at fixed gains', with the '~25 % at the low-gain edge' clause removed.
* 'GLNO itself fires at 96-162 Hz at 5 s inside the window ... higher than any compass type but Delta7' -> '96-145 Hz inside the window, above Delta7 (83-108 Hz) at every window point; only the in-bump EPG mean is higher'.
* Summary-table verdict for gaba 1.75/15: 'dead (3/3 by 2 s)' -> 'dead 3/3 by 5 s; seeds 0 and 2 by 2 s and 1 s, seed 1 only between 3 and 5 s (131-134 Hz, 8/11 above, at 2-3 s)'.
* KEY CLAIM 5 wording: the three seed-1 jump rows share the destination (wedges 3-5, centre 4.1) and the background wedges, not the amplitudes - 133/234/159 (2/25), 144/247/197 (2.25/40), 182/270/238 (2.5/40) vs round-2 base 1.75/15 133/231/160.
* 'a 3-4-wedge bump at wedges 12-14 (225-278 Hz per wedge)' -> 206-287 Hz per wedge (235-287 for the three stiff cases), and the seed-0 pre-pulse bump occurs at every gE >= 2.25 point, not only gD <= 15.
* Answers section, dead gE 1.75 points: 'Delta7 11-20' -> 8.8-20.4 Hz; 'the rest of the brain 0.02-0.05 Hz' at window points -> 0.023-0.037.
* Job metadata: 12-run jobs 367-471 s (6.1-7.9 min), anchor 114 s (1.9 min), from the API records for batch r3-glno-gaba-5f4869.
* 'every field of every row compared, wall_s / cache_dir excluded' is not literally true: config, nt_override and glno_nt also differ between gaba and glu by construction. Say 'every metric field'.
* 'still 7-11x the animal' quotes a ratio whose denominator appears nowhere: no animal E-PG rate is stated in docs/audits/cx_glno.md, cx_wedge.md ('an order of magnitude above the animal's EPG', no number) or elsewhere in docs/. Either cite the rate and its source or keep the qualitative form.
* Minor transcription slips in the hand-built summary table vs the generated one: seed-1 out mean printed as 10 where the per-run table prints 9 (9.5 Hz, 8 rows); bump 152 vs 153 at 1.75/8 seed 2; 227/225/222 vs 227/225/223 at 2.5/25; Delta7 106 vs 107 at 2.5/25 and 96 vs 97 at 2.5/40.
* 'capturable' is thin at three window points: the capture column is 1/1 at gE 1.75/gD 8 and 2/2 at 2/8 and 2/15 (the other seeds had no prior bump elsewhere, and at 2/8 seed 0 a pre-pulse bump at wedges 3-5 overlapping the driven block is classified 'no prior bump' by metrics()). Say how many capture tests each point actually had.
* Process note: I regenerated out/cx_glno_table.{md,csv} while checking that the round-2 report is still reproducible with the edited report(); content is unchanged (same 42 rows) but the new code re-sorts config-major and adds summary columns plus an out/cx_glno_table_summary.csv. I restored both round-2 files from backups and deleted the stray summary CSV; no file of the report's was otherwise edited. My new data are out/sk_base_gE{1.75,2,2.5}.json/.txt, out/sk_gaba_gE2.25.json/.txt, out/sk_gaba_s345.json/.txt, out/sk_base_s345.json/.txt (batch sk-glno-grid-9bf2b1, run dir $CLUSTER_RUNS/sk-glno-grid-9bf2b1).

### Confirmed
* Every shipped table regenerates byte-for-byte. `python scripts/cx_glno.py --report --files "out/cx_glno_gaba_*.json" "out/cx_glno_glu_gE*.json" out/cx_glno_base_r3.json --table cx_glno_gaba_table` reproduces out/cx_glno_gaba_table.md, .csv and _summary.csv identical to the shipped copies (diff -q, 3/3 files). The 51 per-run rows pasted into docs/audits/cx_glno.md section 4 match the generated table in every column (0 differing cells).
* KEY CLAIM 1: gaba and glu are numerically identical at all 24 shared grid points - 0 differing fields over every metric in the JSONs (out/cx_glno_gaba_gE{2,2.25}.json vs out/cx_glno_glu_gE{2,2.25}.json). The logs confirm the mechanism: both compile GLNO sign -1, GLNO->PEN W sum -16371, PEN->GLNO +3496, sum|W| 121,478,280, receptor off. Stronger than claimed: the round-2 out/cx_glno_glu.json rows at gE 1.75/15 and 2/15 are also 0-field-identical to the round-3 gaba rows, so the round-2 glu result transfers exactly.
* KEY CLAIM 2: out/cx_glno_base_r3.json is field-identical to the round-2 base rows at gE 2/gD 15 in 3/3 seeds; its log shows scratch cache None, sum|W| 121,460,584, TYPE_NT_OVERRIDE without GLNO - the cluster's shared cache now carries the round-2 override (it did not in round 2, verification record line 539). My own base runs at fresh seeds 3-5 from the same shared cache give 196.5/191.6/200.5 Hz, reproducing the round-2 skeptic's 196/192/201.
* Reproducibility of the GPU half: I re-ran config gaba at gE 2.25 x gD {8,15,25,40} x seeds 0-2 as an independent job with its own from-raw compile (out/sk_gaba_gE2.25.json, cache_72164311 rebuilt in 35 s, B200). All 12 rows are bit-identical to out/cx_glno_gaba_gE2.25.json in every field. No backend, stale-file or cache confound.
* KEY CLAIM 8 (magnitudes) is exact, recomputed with cx_wedge.effective_weights on the local cache and on the fetched out/cache_72164311: EPG volley onto a PEN +139.81 / +159.79 / +179.78 / +199.72 mV at gE 1.75/2/2.25/2.5, per-cell range 95.8-297.0, PEN fan-in scale 1.000 throughout; Delta7->PEN -45.22 mV at every gE; signed GLNO->PEN -33.0 mV = 23.6 / 20.7 / 18.4 / 16.5 %; Delta7->EPG at gD 15 -341.1 / -335.2 / -328.7 / -322.3 mV. The gaba cache's sum|W| is 121,478,280 vs 121,460,584 adopted, and GLNO is 4 cells, nt gaba, sign -1.
* KEY CLAIM 7 survives replication and now has scatter: at gE 2/gD 15 over six seeds each, base 201.0/201.4/204.3/196.5/191.6/200.5 (mean 199.2, sd 4.5) vs gaba 179.9/181.2/184.4/179.0/175.8/182.3 (mean 180.4, sd 3.0), -9.4 %, ranges disjoint. PEN 46.3-48.7 -> 39.8-41.0, Delta7 98.4-102.7 -> 93.4-96.6, GLNO 126.7-137.2 -> 111.4-118.2.
* The persist verdicts are correctly derived from the stated criterion: recomputing 'in_above >= 8 and out_above <= 3' at t5.0 straight from the 48 gaba JSON rows reproduces the report's yes/no on every row and its per-point counts (3,0,0,0,3,3,2,1,2,3,3,1,2,2,3,2), and every rho = gD/gE^2 in the summary table is right to 0.01. No 'no' row in seeds 0-2 is the 4-cell boundary case, as claimed.
* KEY CLAIM 4 at 5 s: gE 1.75 with gD 15/25/40 is dead in 9/9 runs - in 7.4-11.7 Hz, 0-2 of 11 cells above 22 Hz, PEN 1.4-5.8 Hz, vs 0.10-0.31. The matched silent control makes this a real GLNO effect only at gD 15 (silent 2/3 = 164/42/169 Hz); silent is also 0/3 at gD 25 and 40.
* No default was changed by this task: the diff of scripts/cx_glno.py adds config `gaba`, GRID_GE/GRID_GD, the --no-scratch / --files / --table options and summary columns, leaving GAINS, SEEDS, THRESH_HZ and scratch=True as they were; no flyverse/ file is in the report's FILES. flyverse/brain.py in the working tree now defaults receptor_model='sign' (another round-3 task), but cx_wedge.simulate passes receptor_model explicitly, every run log prints 'receptor off', my replication under that default is bit-identical, and the volley magnitudes are identical under receptor_model None and 'sign' (0 ring entries change) - no confound either way.

## verify:exp:slow  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* docs/audits/slow_term.md section 7 reading 5, 'smell / taste / bitter / dn / motion stay identical within every pair': FALSE on two counts (leaf-by-leaf diff of every sections value in all 11 JSONs, scratchpad pairdiff.py). motion differs in ALL 7 pairs including the three term-off controls, where no slow matrix is built (slow.active false): motion.min_dsi 0.172603788 / 0.167894760 / 0.171274597 in D:/Projects/flyverse/out/r3_slow_abs_ctl_1..3.json, 25-35 of 68 motion leaves differ per pair. bitter differs in the gain 0.01 pair: bitter.shiu_sugar_bitter_MN9_hz 0.0129572 (gain01_1) vs 0.9168104 (gain01_2) - a value the doc's own table prints as 0.01 / 0.92. The task's own scorer already printed it: D:/Projects/flyverse/out/r3_slow_abs_scores.txt last line reads "deterministic keys identical across controls: ... {'motion.min_dsi': False}".
* The inference behind KEY CLAIM 7 / section 7 reading 4 that the paired deterministic shifts are 'bit-identical within each pair, so real' does not hold for walk.power_max under additive 0.02. A third replicate of the identical command (cluster batch vfy-slow-a219ba, D:/Projects/flyverse/out/v_add02_3.json) gives walk.power_max_hz 60.5204, not 52.0497, with power_mean 19.296 / leg 2.398 / GF_max 4.768 - i.e. the add02 pair's agreement at 52.0497 was chance, not reproducibility. (The check still FAILs, so the decision is unaffected; only the 'bit-identical therefore real' reasoning is.)
* docs/audits/slow_term.md section 7, provenance bullet 1 lists 'loom.GF_peak(a) 28.0-28.1' among the values 'bit-identical' to out/r3_default_1..3.json. D:/Projects/flyverse/out/r3_default_2.json has sections.walk.loom.GF_peak_hz = 31.899529. loom.GF_peak is not a deterministic quantity (28.104294 / 28.104294 / 28.042770 across the three controls). The report's KEY CLAIM 1 correctly omits it; the doc sentence should too.

### Corrections
* 'changes only dopamine slow columns' is slightly loose: receptor_groups (613 rows), slow_pos_val (582) and alt_sources (196) also differ between the shipped and dop1r1 tables. All of them are dopamine rows and none is read by connectome.receptor_signs (which consumes only malecns_type, transmitter, fast_sign{sfx}, fast_gain_class{sfx}, slow_sign{ssfx}, slow_gain_class{ssfx} and tier), so there is no model consequence - but say 'only dopamine rows, and of those only the slow and provenance columns'.
* Unseparated confound the section does not flag: every one of the eight active runs carries --dopamine-lead dop1r1 while the control uses the shipped DopEcR table, and no abs + DopEcR + term-on arm exists anywhere in out/ (I enumerated every suite JSON with slow.active true: eight abs runs, all dop1r1; the rest are net_rule class). The round-2 correction in this same file shows the lead alone moves the Shiu checks by up to 40 Hz, so 'the cost is the slow term / the mode / the gain' is not separable from 'the cost is the dop1r1 slow signs'. The decision is unaffected (it is about the settings as run), but reading 2's attribution should be hedged or one DopEcR arm added.
* The non-determinism diagnosis in reading 5 ('The slow update, a sparse-times-dense product per class per step on CUDA, is the only new op') is incomplete. benchmark.py sec_motion (line 439) is an OpticLobe + Brain section with no RNG, no room simulation and, in the controls, no slow matrix at all (slow.active false), yet motion is not bit-reproducible across the three term-off controls. A non-deterministic reduction therefore already exists in the term-off pipeline, and sec_walk's drive comes from the same ol.step_frame; brain._slow_update (line 495-501) and optic's products use the same (M @ x.T).T torch sparse CSR call. The suggested follow-up (rerun the walk section twice under torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)) should cover the optic path too.
* Scope note for the 'optic term' in the gain-semantics section: benchmark.py builds the legacy sections' lobe as optic.OpticLobe(c, r, ctx.optic_params()) at lines 366 (sec_walk) and 443 (sec_motion), with no receptor= and no slow= arguments; only FlyBrain (fly.py line 62) passes receptor and slow. So in the very section where walk.power_max is measured the optic lobe carries neither the abs fast signs nor the monoamine tone, and the 21,850 ol_intrinsic rate units in the structural count get no tone there. This strengthens reading 2's argument (the wing-power change can only arrive through the spiking Brain and its 23 toned DNs) but the sentence '21,850 are optic rate units, i.e. the optic term' should be scoped to the FlyBrain room sections.
* The ranges quoted from two replicates under-sample the scatter. My reruns (out/v_thr01_3.json, out/v_thr01_4.json, out/v_add02_3.json, out/v_gain01_3.json, out/v_ctl_4.json, batch vfy-slow-a219ba) give walk_gf.p99 31.27 (threshold 0.01) against the section's quoted 18.0-26.9, loom_escape.GF_peak 62.43 against 50.2-55.9, and rotation -10.62 against '-9.2 to -10.2'. Every one of them still passes, so runaway-4 now holds in 16 of 16 runs, but the quoted spans should be labelled as two-run ranges.
* The '2 of 2 replicates' bar is the round-3 author's own tightening: slow_term.md 6.2 states the criterion without a replicate count (it was scored on one run per setting there) and only asks for 3 replicates before adoption. The doc says so transparently, and my two extra threshold-0.01 replicates vindicate the tightening (3 of 4 FAIL), so this is a labelling point, not an error.
* The citation 'as test_zero_gains_cost_nothing_and_equal_sign_gain says it should' is one step short: that test pins full-with-zero-gains == 'sign+gain', not == the default 'sign'. The equality to the default holds only because --receptor-gain 1,1,1 sets every gain-class factor to 1, and it is confirmed empirically here by the bit-identity to out/r3_abs_c1..3.json.

### Confirmed
* KEY CLAIM 1 (control = candidate default) CONFIRMED and extended. sections.walk.walk, sections.smell, sections.taste, sections.bitter and sections.dn are bit-identical dicts across all nine of out/r3_slow_abs_ctl_1..3.json, out/r3_abs_c1..3.json and out/r3_default_1..3.json (taste.MN9 10.93417739868164, KC 2.165199041366577 / 816 active, Shiu 139.898498 / 0.817840, calibrated 5.518444 / 0.0, walk.power_max 46.095489501953125, MDN 153, DNa02 2.580632). Tally recomputed from the checks arrays: 27 PASS / 0 FAIL / 2 KNOWN GAP in 3 of 3. I added a fourth control replicate on the cluster in a fresh run dir and cache (out/v_ctl_4.json, cache $CLUSTER_RUNS/vfy-slow-a219ba/cache): 27/0/2 and the same bit-identical walk/smell/taste/bitter/dn. Note r3_default_* runs with --receptor-model off are nonetheless the abs model, because the working-tree brain.py line 136-137 now defaults receptor_model='sign', receptor_net_rule='abs' (the adoption task's diff) and benchmark.py's _apply_receptor is a no-op when the flag is 'off'.
* KEY CLAIM 2 (control scatter) CONFIRMED exactly from the JSONs: walk_gf.p99 19.5766 / 23.9114 / 25.5391, loom_escape.GF_peak 48.4831 / 47.5164 / 57.6474 with 3/3 escapes each, rotation.group_flip -10.0500 / -9.5300 / -9.6610, wind.DNp18 46.4617 / 46.0800 / 44.9400, odour 8cm 17.41 / 17.44 / 17.32 and clean 4.448 / 4.299 / 4.675, motion.min_dsi 0.16789-0.17260, walk.loom.GF_peak 28.0428-28.1043.
* KEY CLAIM 3 (runaway 4 PASS in 11/11) CONFIRMED from the check statuses and criteria (rest.spikes_per_step < 5, walk_gf.p99_hz < 38, loom_escape.GF_peak_hz >= 33, wind.DNp18_flip_hz >= 15): rest 0.0 everywhere, walk_gf 18.00-26.94, loom_escape 47.52-57.65 (active runs 50.23-55.76), wind 44.02-47.74, escapes 3 in every run. My five extra cluster runs keep it at 16/16.
* KEY CLAIM 5 (gain 0.02 breaks Shiu sugar) CONFIRMED: bitter.shiu_sugar_MN9_hz 5.7200 FAIL in out/r3_slow_abs_gain02_1.json and _2.json against criterion '> 50' and a control of 139.8985; additive 0.02 72.0232 PASS, gain 0.01 131.4009 PASS, threshold 0.01 130.3728 PASS. The class-weight comparison also checks out: out/slow_u_gain_02.json Shiu 129.62 with out/slow_u_control_0.json at 6.63.
* KEY CLAIM 6 (tallies) CONFIRMED by recounting each JSON's 29-entry checks array: ctl 27/0/2 x3, add02 26/1/2 x2, gain01 26/1/2 x2, gain02 25/2/2 x2 (walk.power_max + bitter.shiu_sugar), thr01 27/0/2 and 26/1/2. The two KNOWN GAPs are object.LC10a_flip_hz and compass.wedge_cells_persisting in all 11.
* KEY CLAIM 7 (deterministic-section shifts) CONFIRMED numerically: taste.MN9 10.9342 -> 2.4795 / 4.1880 / 7.5361 / 5.0117 and smell KC_hz/active 2.1652/816 -> 1.8852/848, 1.2977/1374, 2.9164/1118, 2.2819/913, bit-identical within every pair and non-monotone in the gain.

## verify:exp:object-null  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* KEY CLAIM 2's blanket negative for LC10b: 'LC11, LC10a, LC10b, LC16, LC4: no signal in either mode (|z| <= 1.0, every U within chance, p >= 0.10)'. Recomputed from their own 20 JSONs with scipy (exact Mann-Whitney, method='exact'): LC10b sign-abs is z = +1.04 (not <= 1.0) and U = 21/25 with exact two-sided p = 0.0952 (not >= 0.10); D:\Projects\flyverse\docs\audits\object_sweep.md 8.4 prints that cell as '0.10' (rounded) while the report's key claim hardens it into 'p >= 0.10'. I then added 4 fresh sign-abs ball seeds (0,5,6,7) and 4 fresh null seeds on the cluster (batch sk3-obj-8b2583, shipped script c8ebd337, all device cuda / B200, out/skr3/): LC10b ball +0.146+-0.064 vs null +0.070+-0.043 (z +1.78, U 14/16). Pooled 9 ball vs 9 null runs: LC10b +0.135+-0.047 vs +0.077+-0.040, Welch t +2.81 p = 0.0127, U 68/81 p = 0.0142. LC10b is above its none-vs-none null on the standard-error and rank tests at 9 replicates per arm, so 'shows nothing above the null' is too strong; it still fails their stated z > 3 criterion (pooled z_SDnull +1.45), and it is NOT above my invisible-ball control (below), so 'no signal at the criterion' survives while 'nothing above the null / every U within chance' does not.

### Corrections
* LC10b sign-abs, exact numbers to use instead of 'every U within chance, p >= 0.10': z +1.04, U 21/25, exact two-sided p = 0.0952 (their 5 seeds); +0.135+-0.047 vs +0.077+-0.040 with Welch +2.81 p 0.0127 / U 68/81 p 0.0142 at 9 replicates per arm (their 5 + my 4). It is still not a demonstrated response: against my invisible-ball control (same ball code path, 0 of 1466 columns changed) LC10b is +0.135+-0.047 vs +0.129+-0.083, Welch +0.10 p 0.93. Suggested wording: 'no type but LPLC2 reaches z > 3; LC10b is the only other type whose direction is consistently positive (z +0.7 off, +1.0 abs; pooled Welch p 0.013) and it does not separate from a zero-stimulus control.'
* The z > 3 criterion is weaker than it looks at n = 5 and should carry that number. Monte Carlo (400k draws, Gaussian null, both arms n): P(z > 3) = 0.0045 per comparison at n = 5 (5.3 % over the 12 comparisons of table 8.4) and 0.012 at n = 4 (13 % over 12). Live example from my replicate: LC16 sign-abs reached z = +4.59 against my own 4-seed null (+6.97 against the invisible-ball null) while their 5 seeds give -0.8 and the pooled 9 v 9 gives -0.21 -- one z > 3 at n ~ 5 is not by itself a signal. LPLC2 does not depend on it (Welch, exact rank-sum, both modes, and an independent batch).
* KEY CLAIM 4 / section 8.1: 'reads above the 7 mV pass criterion' compares a difference statistic with a threshold the script applies to an absolute drive (verdict uses drive_peak_mv > 7 with the ball and not without, probe_object_sweep.py line ~344), not to diff_peak_100ms_mv. The measurement (+11.4 mV null vs +10.8 mV ball) is right; phrase it as 'a difference-peak statistic reaches 11 mV in a stimulus-free pair, above the magnitude the criterion treats as a response'.
* docs/audits/object_sweep.md 8.4: round 2's six off LC11 values (+0.0501 +0.0337 +0.0839 +0.0795 +0.1309 +0.0742, from out/obj/obj_off_s{0,1}.json and out/skobj/sk_off_s0{,_r2,_r3,_r4}.json) average 0.07538 -> '+0.075', not '+0.076'. (Round 2's sign-class '+0.15..+0.48, mean +0.27' is exact: 0.1496-0.4812, mean 0.2694.)
* Cross-file consequence of the flag-default change (not their file to fix, but it is now stale): docs/audits/receptor_integration.md lines 1014-1015 state 'scripts/benchmark.py, probe_loom.py, probe_bitter.py, probe_object_sweep.py, probe_figure_ground.py keep --receptor-model default "off"' and that a no-flag object sweep 'records config.mode: "off"'. As of this change probe_object_sweep.py's flag default is 'default' and a no-flag run records receptor_model 'sign' / 'abs' / flag 'default'. Anyone rerunning a round-2 command line that omitted --receptor-model now gets the shipped model, not off.

The rebuild differs from the shipped file only by the absence of section 6 'Changes against the previous table', which is produced only with --previous (147 lines); I restored the file from a backup and verified it back to sha256 733550218aff438c. ## Completeness critic (round 3)

### Default state

DEFAULT NOW: flyverse/brain.py LIFParams.receptor_model = 'sign', receptor_net_rule = 'abs' (git diff: 14 lines, exactly those two defaults plus the comment block), on flyverse/data/receptors_by_type.csv md5 0381a446107e6050e75cc87b16d7f830 (round-3 contested-flip table, --flip-rule any; docs/audits/receptor_rules.md md5 c72634024536eb6610d870e14b151182, section 3 documents the rule), on the TYPE_NT_OVERRIDE cache (local cache/ and the cluster's shared cache, sum|W| 121,460,584, glutamate cells 29,707). None stays selectable and reproduces the pre-round-3 weights byte for byte (tests/test_receptor_model.py pins md5 2e276b30... for None and f0d145d1... for the default; `pytest -k "none_reproduces or default_is"` = 2 passed today, the cache guard was satisfied so the hash asserts executed; four CPU files 64 passed in 28 s). flyverse/connectome.py's 14-line edit (unlabelled test graphs keep sign(W.data) at tier fallback) is inert on the cache (the adopt skeptic reproduced both hashes with HEAD code). What the default changes: 48,295 of 25,578,600 entries = 179,944 |W| syn = 0.148 % (30,916 glutamate flips / 96,471 syn; 17,379 histamine silencings / 83,473 syn). My CPU split by postsynaptic superclass (connectome.load(); receptor_signs(net_rule='abs') vs sign(W.data)): 44,463 entries / 171,111 syn (95.1 % of the changed |W|) land on ol_intrinsic rate units (T1 62,361 syn, Dm9 25,467, Mi4 20,024 silenced, Mi1 13,013 silenced, ...); 3,832 entries / 8,833 syn land on spiking cells: KCg-m 3,906, DN1pB 1,535, DN1pA 1,100, DN1a 898, KCa'b'-ap2 498, KCg-s1 273, KCa'b'-ap1 272, OA-AL2i3 131 (silenced), TmY14 63 (silenced), everything else < 30 syn.

EVIDENCE FOR IT: 10 suite runs on these weights at 27 PASS / 0 FAIL / 2 KNOWN GAP (out/r3_abs_c1-3.json, out/r3_default_1-3.json, out/r3_default_flagged.json, out/sk3_abs_c4.json, out/sk3_default_4.json, out/sk3_offflag.json -- the last is a default run despite its flag, see below) against off 26/1/2, 24/3/2, 26/1/2, 24/3/2 (out/rm2_off.json, rm2_off_r2, rm2_off_r3, out/skeptic2/rm_off_r4.json). Per-check, the only status changes off -> default are walk.power_max_hz 73.18 FAIL -> 46.10 PASS (bit-deterministic in all 10 vs 4) and loom_escape.GF_peak / escapes (off 31.01 / 28.65 FAIL with 0 escapes in 2 of 4; default 46.87-53.43 with 3/3 escapes in every run); no check is worse in status than in any off run (out/r3_compare.log, out/r3_adopt_compare.log, both 'worse' lists empty, recomputed by both skeptics). Bit-identical Brain-only values across all 10: taste.MN9 10.9342 (off 5.85), Shiu sugar 139.898 (off 123.54), calibrated 5.518 (off 4.57), smell.PN 7.86 / KC_active 816 (off 11.19 / 1426). Probes: demo loom escapes 12/12 seeds at 40.7-55.3 Hz vs off 3/12 at 21.5-35.5; pinned loom DNp01 peak 28-35 Hz with escape in 2 of 6 runs (off 19 Hz, 0/5; round-2 table 37 Hz, 5/5 -- the contested rows carried part of that gain); bitter 3 seeds 139.9/138.9/131.5 (off 123.5/122.0/114.7); figure-ground Spearman 0.95-0.97 with the abs seeds; object sweep FAIL as in every run. Costs on record: legacy loom.GF_peak_hz 27.05-31.90 vs off 36.74-43.88 in 13 vs 4 runs, no overlap (PASS >= 20); smell.KC_active 816 vs 1426; sustain 16 flies x 5 min: hops 24 vs 3 (out/r3_sustain_default.json / _off.json rows: [0,1,1,1,2,2,0,3,1,1,1,2,3,3,1,2] vs [2,0,0,0,1,0,...]; Mann-Whitney U 226, two-sided p 7.7e-5 by the adopt skeptic's scipy recomputation, NOT the 0.0003 the doc prints), path 3.918 vs 3.825 m; meals 0/16 and energy 0.0 in 32/32 flies from t = 80 s, so the feeding assay is degenerate at --energy 0.4.

IS IT JUSTIFIED UNDER THE RULE (data-driven yes, hand-tuned no)? Yes as to kind: the sign comes from absolute receptor expression (abs), the flip stands only when no other profiled source contradicts it (contest_flip, the mirror of the round-2 none_contested rule; 26 abs rows removed: Tm9, L1, 24 ER; 'majority' is identical under abs), the gain class is 'none' (factor 1), no parameter was fitted, and the previous weights are one flag away. The one selection made after scoring is the net rule itself (abs, not class / nonmda): its data-side justification (Nmdar2-led tertile calls exceed the GluCl level; Mi9 / L3 / Tm9 class flips contradicted by every other source) is the round-1 skeptic's and predates the suite scores, and is written in receptor_rules.md section 3 -- that is the part of the record the owner should cite, not the 27/0/2.

Yes as to 'not worse' on everything that runs the model as shipped (the room sections, room_demo, batch_sustain, probe_loom, probe_bitter, probe_figure_ground, probe_object_sweep all go through FlyBrain or pass receptor= explicitly). BUT the two numbers the decision is usually quoted by were measured with the model half applied, a fact no round-3 task or skeptic states (the slow skeptic notes it in passing): scripts/benchmark.py sec_walk (line 366) and sec_motion (line 443) build `optic.OpticLobe(c, r, ctx.optic_params())` with no receptor= / receptor_gain=, while flyverse/fly.py line 62 and scripts/probe_loom.py line 62 pass them. In those sections the Brain carries the abs signs and the rate optic lobe carries NT_SIGN, so walk.* (including walk.power_max_hz, the one FAIL -> PASS), the legacy loom.GF_peak_hz (the one quantified cost) and motion.* are measured with 3,832 entries / 8,833 syn of the default in force (4.9 % of its |W| change: KC, DN1 clock, OA silencings) and the 44,463 optic entries absent. Consequences: (i) the adopt task's open question 4 ('the source of the legacy loom -10 Hz: T1 / Dm9 / histamine silencings, never isolated') is structurally answered -- it cannot be T1 / Dm9 / histamine, which are not applied in that section; it is the KC / DN1 / OA entries -- and the legacy (-10 Hz) and pinned (+9 to +16 Hz) loom results disagree in direction because only the pinned probe applies the optic side; (ii) the retire task's decisive check (walk.power_max 51.56 / 69.77 / 72.8-79.3 vs 46.10) is the same half-applied section; (iii) 27/0/2 is not yet shown to be a property of the shipped model. The fix is two lines plus a 6-job batch (follow-up 1); until it is run, the honest statement is: adopted on 'no status regression in 3/3 (10/10) suite runs and every fully-applied probe', with walk.power_max and loom.GF_peak flagged as pending re-measurement. Second live defect: `benchmark.py --receptor-model off` is a silent no-op (Context._apply_receptor returns when the flag is off, so LIFParams keeps sign/abs and the JSON header records model null -- out/sk3_offflag.json is a 27/0/2 default run); probe_figure_ground.py (line 35) and screen_rotation.py (line 30) have the same 'off leaves the class alone' semantics; the off baselines in out/rm2_off*.json predate the default change and stay valid only because None reproduces HEAD's weights byte for byte. No off suite run can be produced from the shipped scripts today.

### Assessment

Round 3 did every item on the NT_INTEGRATION section-7 list and all eight round-2 follow-ups that were actionable (the ninth, the native slow kernel, was conditional on a slow-term keeper that did not materialise), and the round's one default change is the right kind under the project's rule: a sign taken from absolute receptor expression with a symmetric contested-flip rule, no gain factor, no fitted parameter, reversible by one flag with a byte-for-byte test. The five skeptics returned 'mostly sound' on all five tasks and every reproduction they attempted succeeded (three byte-identical table rebuilds; 4th and 5th suite replicates at 27/0/2; the retire, compass, slow and object batches re-run with matching numbers); the refutations are attribution and wording errors (per-seed loom determinism, 'narrower' compass window, 'discrete' walk.power_max values, 'p >= 0.10', the 0.0003 p-value, 'stay identical within every pair'), none of which reverses a decision, and the skeptics added the controls the tasks lacked (seed-matched silent GLNO grid, invisible-ball null, extra replicates) which in every case supported the direction of the finding. What the round did not catch, and what qualifies the headline: scripts/benchmark.py builds the optic lobe of its walk and motion sections without the receptor lookup, so the check that turns 26/1/2 into 27/0/2 (walk.power_max) and the one quantified cost (loom.GF_peak -10 Hz) were produced by 8,833 synapses onto KC / DN1 / OA cells with 95 % of the default absent from that section -- also the decisive check of the retire study. The adoption's 'not worse' verdict stands on the fully-applied room sections and probes, but the 27/0/2 number should not be quoted as a property of the shipped model until round-4 item 1 is run, and the open question 'where does the -10 Hz come from' is now structurally narrowed to the Brain-side entries. Second, the round's scripts cannot produce an off suite run any more (benchmark.py --receptor-model off is a no-op), which makes the four round-2 off JSONs the only baseline and makes item 1 urgent for the next skeptic. Third, the one behavioural change no check scores -- spontaneous take-offs 24 vs 3 in a room with no loom -- was measured once and should be replicated with a feeding-capable sustain before the default is relied on by the sustain / RL work. Compass: the session-9 conclusions (ring-attractor wiring under the model's rules; Delta7 gain on Delta7 -> EPG only; the ExR / ER loop as the closing gain; 200 Hz bump) survive; the GLNO=gaba scan and the skeptic's control change only the gain-window statement (equal totals, shifted one gD step, seed-0 window narrowed at 3 seeds) and leave gE 2 / gD 15 inside both windows, so no compass default moves and the next compass work is the session-9 dynamics list. Slow term and hunger remain blocked on the data (walk.power_max cost in 11/12 runs), and the object item is closed with an error bar. Reporting debt is of the same class as round 2 and is listed with exact line numbers; the round produced no untracked files and 64 passing tests.

"python -c 'import torch; assert torch.cuda.is_available()' && CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=:4096:8 python scripts/benchmark.py --deterministic --eager --sections walk,motion --seeds 0,1,2 --receptor-model full --receptor-net-rule abs --receptor-gain 1,1,1 --slow-mode threshold --slow-gain-monoamine 0.01 --dopamine-lead dop1r1 --json out/r4_thr01_det_1.json > out/r4_thr01_det_1.txt; cat out/r4_thr01_det_1.txt"` x3, plus the term-off control x2 (`--slow-gain-monoamine 0`) and one DopEcR arm x2 (threshold 0.01 without --dopamine-lead). Settles: whether walk.power_max 36.79 / 53.56 / 53.56 / 53.56 under threshold 0.01 (and motion.min_dsi's spread in the controls) is a reduction-order artefact, and whether the walk.power_max cost belongs to the term or to the dop1r1 slow signs. Only if a setting then keeps every control PASS in 3/3 is the native slow kernel (round-2 follow-up 8) worth writing.

# Round 4: adversarial verification record

Round 4 (workflow `wf_4e83e37d-2a2`: rescore, five experiments, six skeptics (Opus), one critic). Verdicts verbatim.
The rescore task returned before its batch landed; the skeptic and critic recovered the data (`out/r4_*.json`) and
the owner wrote the re-score section of `receptor_integration.md` from them. Corrections applied as 'Corrections
(round-4 verification)' sections in each document; the two script defects (retire_measures off/default,
compare_suite_runs round4 guard and list) fixed in the same commit.

## verify:exp:sustain  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* CLAIM 13 + doc S.1 premise, 'every airborne transition is spontaneous' / sec_walk_gf's voluntary_takeoffs counter is 'exactly spontaneous hops' -- REFUTED on the cluster. flyverse/batch_body.py step() lines 76-86 launch by EITHER route: escape = gf>=gf_threshold (33 Hz, body.Flight.launch(escape=True), a ballistic jump_speed jump) or voluntary = power>=50 Hz held 0.3 s (launch(escape=False)). batch_sustain.py's `hops` counts both and the JSONs carry no split, so nothing in the round-4 artefacts supports 'spontaneous'. My run dir $CLUSTER_RUNS/sk4-hoproute-85ee51 (5 jobs, 0 failed, 23.3 min, every log 'device=cuda'), scripts/probe_hop_route.py at the identical command/seeds (brain seed 0, env 0-15, 16 x 300 s): default 24 hops = 10 ESCAPE + 14 voluntary (out/sk4_route_default_1.json); off 2 hops = 2 escape + 0 voluntary (out/sk4_route_off_1.json). Per-row walking-GF maximum, default median 33.04 Hz / mean 40.14 / max 99.56 with 8 of 16 rows at or above the 33 Hz escape threshold, vs off median 28.51 / max 37.55 with 2 of 16 (Mann-Whitney U 214.0, p 1.27e-03). So ~40 % of the default's room take-offs are GF escape jumps on room optic flow, not spontaneous take-offs, and the default's GF tail is what crosses the threshold.
* CLAIM 13/15 + doc S.3, that benchmark.py's existing gf_hz=1e9 counter is the right instrument for this cost and that its reference pair is 'off 0.76 / default 5.14 hops per 1,000 fly-s' -- REFUTED by the arm that runs exactly that instrument. out/sk4_route_default_nogf.json (--gf-hz 1e9, i.e. sec_walk_gf's own trick, same seeds, 4,800 fly-s): 10 hops, all voluntary, 0 escape -- down from 24 with the route live (Mann-Whitney U 192.0, p_asym 0.0113, p_exact 0.0152). On that instrument the two conditions read default 2.08 per 1,000 fly-s (10/4,800) and off 0.00 (0 voluntary hops in 4,800 fly-s), not 5.14 and 0.76. The Poisson power table in out/r4_hopcheck_power.log (E 24.7 vs 3.67 at 4,800 fly-s, best bound K=11, and the >= 2,400 fly-s sizing conclusion) is therefore calibrated on rates the proposed check would not produce; the proposed Ref bounds (< 50 storm guard, < 2.0 gap entry) sit on the same mis-specified scale. The instrument would still separate the models (10 vs 0), but its reference numbers and its bound must be re-derived on voluntary-only counts.
* CLAIM 11, that the +2.1 % path length is a second replicated effect -- REFUTED as an independent effect (the direction replicates, the independence does not, and the report never tested it). Pooled OLS of path on condition + hops (n=96): condition +0.0444 +- 0.0361 m, t +1.23, p 0.22; hops +0.0242 +- 0.0158 m/hop, t +1.54, p 0.13 -- the hop term absorbs 0.0318 of the raw 0.0762 m difference. Among flies with zero hops the difference is 3.6391 +- 0.0723 (default, n=10) vs 3.6042 +- 0.1061 m (off, n=39), Mann-Whitney U 222.0, p 0.51. My independent 4th pair gives +1.72 % at p_asym 0.486 (out/sk4_sustain_default_4.json / sk4_sustain_off_4.json). A ballistic escape jump adds displacement, so path and hops are collinear here and the 2 % cannot be quoted as a separate cost.

### Corrections
* The round-3 critic's follow-up 3 says: 'If the take-off excess replicates, ADD a walk_gf-style check for spontaneous hops to benchmark.py so the cost is scored, not just noted' (docs/audits/receptor_verification.md line 829). The excess replicates on the report's own criterion and in my 4th pair, and nothing was added -- the report's DECISION states this but justifies it only on wall time. The defensible reason it does not give is the SHARED-FILE RULE: scripts/benchmark.py is another round-4 task's file this round (its working-tree diff is that task's sec_walk/sec_motion receptor fix plus a --deterministic flag). State the deviation as 'the check is owed and was left to the owner of benchmark.py', not as a cost/benefit choice.
* '0 in all 13 round-2/round-3 suite JSONs' -- the list that follows names 14 files (3 + 1 + 3 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 3 + 1) and voluntary_takeoffs is 0 in all 14. Also, the counter is not identically zero across the project: of the 195 suite JSONs under out/ that carry a walk_gf section, 8 have a non-zero value, including out/retire/no_cap.json at 41 with receptor_model None and out/retire_cluster/no_cap.json at 1 for the same configuration -- so the instrument has dynamic range but is itself wildly non-reproducible (1 vs 41). Say '14 files' and mention the no_cap 1-vs-41 scatter when proposing it as a scored check.
* The round-1 'full' ceiling is not 2,070 per 1,000 fly-s: out/rm_full.json does record 31 takeoffs in 15 s (2,067), but out/out/v_round1params.json records 35 in 15 s = 2,333 per 1,000 fly-s. Quote the ceiling as '>= 2,330 (31-35 hops in 15 s of one fly)'.
* Every 'p_exact' in the report is computed by scipy's method='exact', which is only valid without ties, and these data are massively tied (hops takes 5 distinct values over 96 flies; final energy is exactly 0 in 88 of 96). A tie-corrected Monte-Carlo permutation test on the ranks (200,000 resamples) gives hops p 1e-05 / 0.00468 / 0.00034 per batch and 0/200,000 pooled -- so the hop conclusion is untouched -- but final energy comes out at 0.0722, against the asymptotic 0.124 and the quoted p_exact 0.463. Drop the 0.463 (and the 0.707 / 0.718 for meals and min energy): they overstate the nullity of the energy comparisons, which are better described as 'not significant, p 0.07-0.12'.
* The fixed-seed scatter was measured with 2 replicates, below the task's '>= 3 replicates' rule, and the quoted range is too narrow. Three identical-command runs at brain seed 0 / env 0-15 now exist: default 21 (out/r4_sustain_default_1.json), 17 (_1b), 24 (out/sk4_route_default_1.json, my run) and off 1, 0, 2. Quote 'default 17-24, off 0-2 over three identical-seed runs' instead of '17-21 / 0-1'; the gap is a factor of ~20 either way. (Caveat: my third run is scripts/probe_hop_route.py, which reproduces batch_sustain's setup and loop and adds only read-only bookkeeping plus a launch wrapper.)
* CLAIM 6's seed-0 'pooled 32 v 32 ... U 900.0, p 2.92e-09' is pseudo-replication: the two default batches and the two off batches share the same brain seed AND the same 16 environment seeds, so it is 16 flies measured twice, not 32 flies. Report it as a consistency check, not as a p-value.
* CLAIM 5's Kruskal-Wallis is not a brain-seed test: the three batches differ in brain RNG and in environment seeds together (the doc's S.0 says so correctly). Call it 'no detectable batch effect (brain RNG and environment seeds confounded)'. Separately, the conservative batch-level test is worth stating since 16 flies share one batched brain: paired t on the three batch totals p 0.0052, Wilcoxon/sign p 0.25 (the n=3 floor).
* Provenance that is in no fetched file and cannot be checked here: run dirs r4-sustain-f2857e / r4-sustain-rep-c6d643, '6 jobs / 0 failed / 44.2 min', '2 jobs / 0 failed / 25.7 min', and 'every job log prints cuda ok NVIDIA B200'. What the artefacts do show is 'device=cuda' in all eight .txt logs, and the wall times are consistent with the claimed batch durations (max 2,565.9 s = 42.8 min and 1,471.3 s = 24.5 min). For comparison my own batch is checkable end to end: $CLUSTER_RUNS/sk4-hoproute-85ee51, 5 jobs, 0 failed, 23.3 min, all five logs 'device=cuda'.
* PROCESS: the doc's S.0 says 'Generator: scripts/compare_sustain_runs.py (logs out/r4_sustain_compare.log, out/r4_sustain_replicate.log, out/r4_sustain_energy.log, out/r4_hopcheck_power.log)'. It generates only the first two; nothing in scripts/ produces the energy log or the power table (grep for 'first frame with all', 'best bound K', 'Poisson power' over scripts/ returns nothing), so two of the report's key claims (9 and 15, plus the dispersion in 16) rest on scratchpad-only code, against the 'ship every generator into scripts/' rule. Either ship the generator or fold those two outputs into compare_sustain_runs.py.
* CLAIM 15's wall-time range '25-43 min' for 4,800 fly-s excludes the fastest observed job: the eight runs span 1,018.0-2,565.9 s = 17.0-42.8 min (out/r4_sustain_off_1.json at 4.71 fly-s/wall-s). Quote 17-43 min, and note that the spread is GPU contention, not model.
* Small scale points worth fixing: the off reference '0.76 per 1,000 fly-s' uses 14,400 fly-s and omits the fixed-seed rerun, which had 0 hops (over 4 batches it is 0.57); 'the first flies reach 0 between t=120 and 180 s' is really between 170 and 180 s in all six jobs; and '15 fly-seconds ... expects 0.077 hops' becomes 0.031 on the voluntary-only rate the proposed instrument would actually measure.
* A second existing hop counter the report missed, which its S.3 survey should include: scripts/benchmark.py sec_loom_escape records seeds[*].hops_before_loom over the 5 s walk phase with the GF escape route LIVE at 33 Hz (lines 487-505), and it is also unscored and also 0 -- 0 over 15 seeds x 5 s = 75 fly-s under sign/abs and 0 over 27 seeds x 5 s = 135 fly-s under off, where the measured rates expect 0.39 and 0.10. It is the closer analogue of what the room runs measure (both routes) and is the better base for the proposed section; the walking GF max it records never exceeds 26.5 Hz in 42 single-fly seeds, against the median 33.0 Hz my 16-fly batch shows, so the single-fly sections also under-sample the GF tail.

### Confirmed
* THE DECISION ITSELF -- the take-off excess REPLICATES -- CONFIRMED and independently strengthened. My own 4th brain RNG at untested seeds (scripts/batch_sustain.py --seed 3 --seeds 48..63, same command, out/sk4_sustain_default_4.json vs sk4_sustain_off_4.json): 21 vs 5 hops, 1.3125 +- 1.4009 vs 0.3125 +- 0.6021 per fly, Mann-Whitney U 190.0, p_asym 0.0108, p_exact 0.0189. ## verify:exp:retire  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* CLAIM 3, 'walk.power_max and motion.min_dsi are bit-identical across all 6 runs (zero scatter)': REFUTED on both halves. (a) motion.min_dsi takes three distinct values in those very 6 JSONs -- 0.2333961144552274 (out/retire_r4/baseline_r1, baseline_r2, out/r4_rescore_fetch/r4_default_1.json), 0.2333960706308492 (baseline_r3, r4_default_2.json), 0.23339589533336752 (r4_default_3.json). (b) walk.power_max is NOT deterministic: I ran an 8th full-suite baseline with a byte-identical config (I diffed all 15 config keys of out/sk4_retire/baseline_r4/baseline.json against out/retire_r4/baseline_r1/baseline.json: zero differences; same NVIDIA B200; run dir $CLUSTER_RUNS/sk4-retire2-fdf854) and got walk.power_max_hz = 80.09278869628906 (+0.628 Hz) with walk.power_sustained_hz = 39.90780506134034 (+2.018 Hz) and motion.min_dsi 0.23374141573800902. Status was still 26/1/2 with the same single FAIL, so the verdict is unaffected, but 'zero scatter' is a 6-sample artefact.
* CLAIM 3, 'loom.GF_peak takes two values (50.3826 x4, 50.8586 x2)': REFUTED. My full-suite baseline (out/sk4_retire/baseline_r4/baseline.json) gives loom.GF_peak_hz = 54.26889419555664, a third value in full-suite draws (range 50.38-54.27, 3.9 Hz). Adding six walk-section baseline draws (out/sk4_walkrep/rep1..6/baseline.json, run dir $CLUSTER_RUNS/sk4-walkrep-237ad4: 46.80375671386719, 50.38260269165039, 50.38260269165039, 46.889095306396484, 46.889095306396484, 46.9710693359375) the shipped default's legacy loom.GF_peak spans 46.80-54.27 (~7.5 Hz), not the 0.48 Hz the report and docs/audits/anti_runaway.md line 390 quote.
* CLAIM 14 (DETERMINISM) 'every walk.* and smell.* value and motion.min_dsi (except gf_damping_dnp70 and pair_gain_lpi_x1/x3, which vary in the 4th decimal) is identical across all three replicates of each configuration -- three identical reruns, so the claim is backed': REFUTED three ways from the 21 shipped JSONs plus my reruns. (i) motion.min_dsi differs between replicates in ALL SEVEN configurations, not four: baseline 0.2333961144552274/0.2333961144552274/0.2333960706308492; no_gf_damping 0.241096463199512/0.241096463199512/0.2410964184151673; pair_gain_lpi_x2 0.24374286627421374/0.24374283164279192/0.24374287582109427; no_al_ln_override 0.2397427005762057/0.23974259329551598/0.23973578052598304. The 'identical' reading is an artefact of the 4-decimal rounding in the task's own out/r4_retire_summary.json. (ii) gf_damping_dnp70's motion.min_dsi varies in the 3rd decimal (0.24510227490776387 / 0.24595005169926143 / 0.2395259208324488), not the 4th. (iii) the baseline's walk.power_max_hz and walk.power_sustained_hz are not reproducible (80.0928 / 39.9078 in my 8th draw), so 'a deterministic claim requires two identical reruns' is not met for the deciding check.
* CLAIM 11, 'the tightest check is walk.power_max itself -- +1.519 Hz for no_gf_damping and gf_damping_dnp70 ... the next tightest are odour.apple_clean (+1.37 to +1.65)': REFUTED. Computing the per-check PASS margin from each criterion string in all 9 passing-config JSONs: odour.apple_channel_clean_hz (bound '<= 6') has the SMALLER margin in two of the three configurations -- 1.5047 Hz for no_gf_damping (4.495302677154541 in r1) and 1.3746 Hz for gf_damping_dnp70 (4.6254096031188965 in r2) against walk.power_max's 1.5195 Hz -- and bitter.calibrated_sugar_bitter_MN9_hz (0.0 against '< 1') has a 1.00 Hz margin in all three. walk.power_max is only the tightest for pair_gain_lpi_x2. The odour range is also +1.3746 to +1.6743, not '+1.37 to +1.65'.
* CLAIM 11 / docs/audits/anti_runaway.md line 479 ('a check whose value is bit-identical across replicates (zero scatter)') and the same section's caveat (3) ('the scatter quoted for walk.power_max is exactly zero and must not be read as a confidence interval'): REFUTED as a statement of fact by the 80.09278869628906 draw. The direction of the caveat is right; the number is wrong.
* CLAIM 13, 'the round-3 PN-named 42,005 that could not be reproduced is 45,222 |W| over 2,662 edges under a prefix/suffix name rule': NOT REPRODUCIBLE, and no generator was shipped. Recomputing on the same local cache from the 27 cells the regex relabels (isolated by recompiling with connectome.UNKNOWN_NT_OVERRIDE_REGEX = {} into out/sk4_cache_no_al_ln and diffing neurons.nt), their output onto name-selected targets is: type endswith 'PN' 1,488 edges / 37,349 |W|; 'PN' substring 1,488->2,779 edges / 48,982 |W|; 'PN' prefix 0 / 0; instance substring 'PN' 2,779 / 48,982; class == 'ALPN' 2,893 / 49,483; class in {ALPN, ALON} 2,965 / 51,360. Twelve rules tried, none yields 2,662 edges / 45,222 |W|. So 'all round-3 structural numbers reproduce' is true of every other figure but not of this one, and the replacement for the retracted 42,005 is itself unverifiable.

### Corrections
* The margin the retirement rests on must be restated. Because the baseline's own walk section is not bit-reproducible (80.09278869628906 / 39.90780506134034 in one of 8 full-suite baseline draws, out/sk4_retire/baseline_r4/baseline.json, byte-identical config), 'a +1.52 Hz margin with zero measured scatter' should read '+1.52 Hz against a measured walk-section run-to-run excursion of up to 0.63 Hz on walk.power_max and 2.02 Hz on walk.power_sustained (1 of 8 baseline draws)'. The retirement still clears its criterion -- no_gf_damping returned 48.48052978515625 in 10 of 10 draws across two independent batches -- but the margin is ~2.4x the largest observed excursion, not infinite, and docs/audits/anti_runaway.md's caveat (3) ('the scatter ... is exactly zero') needs the same fix.
* 'LPi share of LPLC2's shaped input 2.16 / 4.22 / 6.20 / 8.10 % at x1/x2/x3/x4' (claim 13 and docs/audits/anti_runaway.md line 438) mislabels the quantity: out/r4_lpi_scan_structure.json's rule applies the LPi pair gain but not the T4/T5 -> * x2 pair gain that is in force in the same optic lobe (optic.DEFAULT_PAIR_GAIN line 91). With every gain applied the shares are 1.63 / 3.21 / 4.74 / 6.22 % (out/sk4_retire_structure.txt, key 'share_all_gains'); the quoted numbers are the share of the RAW input with only the LPi factor applied. Either relabel or use the all-gains figure. The companion ratio 'LPi : (T4/T5 x2) = 0.033 / 0.067 / 0.100 / 0.134' is correct as stated.
* scripts/retire_measures.py's docstring -- in the file this task edited -- still asserts the opposite of the task's own claim 12 and was not fixed: lines 23-28 say '--receptor-model ... default to `off`, which does NOT mean "no receptor model": benchmark.Context leaves LIFParams alone when the flag is off, so a run inherits brain.LIFParams' own defaults (round 3: receptor_model 'sign', receptor_net_rule 'abs') ... Pass `--receptor-model off` explicitly plus a LIFParams patch only if you want the pre-round-3 weights.' Verified false in-process: Context.receptor_model maps 'off' -> None and _apply_receptor sets p.receptor_model = None, so a plain `python scripts/retire_measures.py` run now scores the PREVIOUS model, silently. Fix the docstring (and consider changing the argparse default from 'off' to a 'default' choice, as benchmark.py already has).
* The generator of out/r4_lpi_scan_structure.json and out/r4_gf_damping_structure.json was not shipped into scripts/ -- the exact defect the round-3 critic raised about out/r3_retire_structure{,2}.json, and a violation of 'ship every generator you write into scripts/'. I wrote D:\Projects\flyverse\scripts\skeptic_retire_r4_structure.py (new file, not an edit of theirs; it compiles the regex-emptied connectome into out/sk4_cache_no_al_ln and never touches the shared cache) and every figure except the PN weight reproduces from it.
* Claim 9's 'walk_gf.p99 falls with it [monotonically across the scan]' is not supported by the data: the per-replicate ranges overlap heavily (x1 26.98-31.14 vs x2 23.63-31.09) and walk_gf.p99's own 3-replicate spread is 4-11 Hz within a configuration (e.g. gf_damping_dnp70 15.67-27.02). Only walk.GF_max is monotone across x1..x4. State walk_gf.p99 as 'lower at x3/x4 than at x1/x2, within a 4-11 Hz replicate spread'.
* Claim 4's 'the default is ~11 Hz BETTER than off on loom.GF_peak' keeps its direction but not its precision once the default's real scatter is counted: default 46.80-54.27 over 8 draws (mine + theirs) against off 38.16-40.83 (round 4) and 36.74-43.88 (round 2), i.e. a 3-17 Hz separation, not ~11 Hz.
* One batch figure could not be checked against any file: '12.3 min wall'. Everything else about the batch is corroborated (run dir present on the cluster with all 21 outputs, 'rc 0' in all 21 .txt, per-run 6.39-10.91 min from total_runtime_s, B200 in all 21, md5 match between cluster and local copies).
* Confound checked and cleared: flyverse/body.py was modified in the working tree at the start of my session and is clean now (another task reverted it), so the r4-retire batch may have shipped a different body.py than HEAD. It did not matter for the deciding number -- my benchmark.py walk run on today's tree returns walk.power_max_hz 79.46501159667969, bit-identical to the report's baseline -- so the wing-power model behind walk.power_max is unchanged.

## verify:exp:compass  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* CLAIM 7 / cx_glno.md sec 5 Answers: "vs 0.72-0.80" at the three robust points is wrong as scoped. Over the 36 runs at (2,15), (2.25,25), (2.5,25) in both conditions the raw t5.0_vector_strength range is 0.7296 (gaba 2.25/25 seed 4) to 0.7967 (base 2.5/25 seed 2), i.e. 0.73-0.80. 0.72 is the min of a DIFFERENT set -- the silent-ring no-failure set in cx_wedge.md, whose min is 0.7198 at base gE 2 / gD 25 seed 4, a point that is NOT robust to the GLNO sign. Their own section-5 table is self-consistent (vs_min column 0.73-0.77 across the six robust cells), so the prose contradicts the table it summarises. Verified in out/sk4_glno_verify.txt and by direct reads of out/sk_base_gE2.json / out/cx_glno_gaba_gE2.25.json.
* CLAIM 12: "my working-tree diff is 3 files, 256 insertions, 0 deletions" is wrong. `git diff --numstat -- scripts/cx_glno.py docs/audits/cx_glno.md docs/audits/cx_wedge.md` = 104 + 119 + 36 = 259 insertions, 0 deletions, 3 files. The rest of claim 12 is confirmed (see confirmed list).
* CLAIM 1's batch-level figures "0 failed, 8.3 min" are not verifiable from anything in the repo: no cluster log for r4-glno-d04de8 was shipped (out/ has r3_cluster.log, sk3_cluster.log, r4_slowdet_cluster.log but no r4 GLNO batch log), so those two numbers rest on console output I cannot see. What IS verifiable: the run dir name is corroborated from inside the data (cache_dir '$CLUSTER_RUNS/r4-glno-d04de8/out/cache_72164311' in out/r4_gaba_s345b.json), the three per-job wall times 320 / 433 / 206 s and row counts 12 / 15 / 9 are the last lines of out/r4_base_gE2.25.txt, out/r4_gaba_s345b.txt, out/r4_base_s345b.txt, the 79 s from-raw compile is in the gaba log, and all three logs print 'device NVIDIA B200; torch 2.11.0+cu128'. 8.3 min is plausible (my own 4-job batch with a 338 s longest job took 10.0 min) but it is an unsourced number.

### Corrections
* vs range at the three robust points: 0.730-0.797, not 0.72-0.80. Fix the prose in the report's claim 7 and in cx_glno.md section 5 ('Answers', first paragraph). cx_wedge.md's 'vs 0.72-0.80' is correct as written because it describes the silent-ring no-failure set, whose min is 0.7198 at base 2/25 seed 4.
* Working-tree diff: 3 files, 259 insertions, 0 deletions (104 scripts/cx_glno.py + 119 docs/audits/cx_glno.md + 36 docs/audits/cx_wedge.md), not 256.
* Percentage ranges are rounded inward. PEN is -14.4 to -16.2 % (not '-14 to -16'), Delta7 -4.3 to -6.4 % (not '-4 to -6'), GLNO -12.3 to -14.4 % (not '-12 to -14'). Per point: PEN -15.7 / -16.2 / -14.4; Delta7 -5.2 / -6.4 / -4.3; GLNO -13.3 / -14.4 / -12.3.
* The vector-strength means quoted (0.763, 0.767) are computed on vs already rounded to 2 dp by metrics(); the raw-value means are 0.762 +- 0.0149 and 0.769 +- 0.0133. Immaterial to 'within scatter', but the sd for 2.25/25 base is 0.013 raw, not 0.012.
* 'outside mean likewise 10.2/9.5/8.9/10.0/11.4/11.2 Hz' is exact only to 1 dp: the out COUNT is exactly constant within a seed, the out RATE is not (1-3 distinct values per seed, differing in the 4th significant figure, e.g. seed 2: 8.910334 / 8.932256 / 8.934561). The count is the load-bearing half of the claim and it is exact.
* Six cells of the cx_glno.md section-5 table differ by one unit in the last displayed digit from raw-value aggregation, because the generator aggregates metrics() output that is already rounded (round-then-mean): 1.75/8 base bump range min 170 vs 171 (raw min 170.5), 1.75/8 gaba bump range max 154 vs 155, 2/25 base PEN 44.9 vs 45.0, 2.25/15 base PEN 58.5 vs 58.4, 2.5/25 base GLNO 159.8 vs 159.9, 2.5/25 gaba Delta7 107.2 vs 107.3 and GLNO 140.2 vs 140.3. Every persist / boundary / confined / per-seed in_above / per-seed out_above / per-seed bump_hz / vs cell matches exactly.
* cx_glno.md section 5: 'the lowest six-seed robust bump is 180 Hz (gaba at gE 2 / gD 15)' is a cell mean; the lowest single run at the robust points is 175.8 Hz (gaba 2/15 seed 4). The order-of-magnitude point stands.
* cx_wedge.md failure taxonomy: gE 2 / gD 40 seed 1 is listed under class (b) 'a jump off the driven tile ... at high rho', but its 5 s state is in 7.6 Hz, 0 of 11 cells, with a pre-pulse bump elsewhere (pre out 23.6 Hz, 11 of 35) surviving at centre 9.28 -- mechanistically class (a), an undisplaceable bump, and 'not captured' by their own rule. Defensible only via the clause's 'or an outright death'; better moved or relabelled.
* Reporting gap, not a number: neither the report nor either doc states that every run in the grid was made with receptor_model=None while LIFParams now defaults to 'sign'/'abs'. That premise is true but was untested and is table-dependent -- the only sign-abs ring data previously on record, out/cx_glno_sign-abs.json (round-2 table, before the 24 contested ER ring rows were removed), differs from base in 78-83 fields per run and flips gE 1.75 / gD 15 seed 1 from base's jump (in 42.0, centre 4.1, 3 of 11) to a held bump (in 161.4, centre 1.2, 9 of 11). My 27-run check (out/sk4_signabs_robust.json, out/sk4_signabs_fail.json) closes it on the shipped table; cx_glno.md section 5 and the cx_wedge.md replacement table should say 'receptor model off == the shipped sign/abs default on this ring (verified, out/sk4_glno_receptor_check.txt: 0 of 27,553 ring-core entries changed)' so no future table change silently invalidates the grid.
* Ship the batch log. 'r4-glno-d04de8, 3 jobs, 0 failed, 8.3 min' has no file behind it; add the cluster_run console output as out/r4_glno_cluster.log the way round 3 did (out/r3_cluster.log).
* Disclosure of my own side effects (no report file edited, all mtimes unchanged): I added scripts/verify_cx_glno_r4.py (independent loader; the verification is not routed through their seed_table) and wrote out/sk4_glno_verify.txt, out/sk4_glno_receptor_check.txt, out/sk4_glno_cluster.log, out/sk4_{base,gaba}_rep.{json,txt}, out/sk4_signabs_{robust,fail}.{json,txt}, out/sk4_glno_seeds_regen.*. Two pre-existing gitignored scratch files were rewritten with byte-identical or equivalent content: out/cx_glno_gaba_table.{md,csv,_summary.csv} by rerunning their --report (diffed clean against copies taken first) and out/cx_glno_receptor_check.json by my --check-receptor run (it held the round-2 numbers; it now holds the shipped-table numbers). Neither is in the report's FILES list.

## verify:exp:ntmaj  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* CLAIM 4, baseline identity: out/r3_default_{1,2,3}.json do NOT record the shipped default. config.receptor = {'model': None} with no 'flag' key, and out/r3_default_1.txt prints no receptor line at all (out/r3_default_flagged.txt, same job r3-adopt-63e3de, prints 'receptor model sign (abs); fast sign changed on 48,295'). By the header check the task prescribes, the report's baseline is indistinguishable from an off run. It IS a default (sign/abs) run, but only by value fingerprint, which I verified bit-for-bit: r3_default_1 vs r3_default_flagged taste.MN9 10.93417739868164 = 10.93417739868164, smell.KC_active 816 = 816, bitter.shiu 139.89849777221679 = 139.89849777221679, walk.power_max 46.095489501953125 = 46.095489501953125, while off (out/r4_rescore_fetch/r4_off_1.json, header {'model': None, 'flag': 'off'}) gives 5.85 / 1426 / 123.54. The report states the identity as fact without noting that the named file's header contradicts it.
* CLAIM 4, 'out/r4_default_*.json did not exist in out/ at scoring time': false as an availability claim. out/r4_rescore_fetch/r4_default_{1,2,3}.json (config.receptor model 'sign', net_rule 'abs', flag 'default', fast_sign_changed_entries 48295, cache $CLUSTER_RUNS/r4-rescore-680005/cache, nt unknown 2,361 - i.e. the fixed-benchmark default on the shipped cache) have mtime 2026-09-12 01:28:29, seven minutes BEFORE the scoring pass that wrote out/r4_type_majority.csv (01:35:48) and docs/audits/nt_type_majority.md (01:35:52). Only the glob path out/r4_default_*.json was empty.
* CLAIM 8, 'the only shared-code edit is md_table iterating over df.astype(object)': git diff -U0 -- scripts/audit_nt.py shows TWO modified existing lines: `for _, r in df.iterrows()` -> `df.astype(object).iterrows()` and `log(f"neurons {N:,}")` -> `log(f"neurons {N:,}  sum|W| {int(np.abs(c.W.data).sum()):,}")` (the default audit path's stdout). Everything else is addition. The md output is unaffected: diff docs/audits/nt_audit.md out/nt_audit_regen_check.md differs only at line 3 (date 2026-09-11 -> 2026-09-12), so the second half of claim 8 stands.

### Corrections
* CRITERION: against the right baseline the criterion is MET, not 'NOT MET'. My cross-tab (scripts/skeptic_ntmaj_verify.py --scores/--baselines) of out/r4_ntmaj_{1,2,3}.json vs out/r4_rescore_fetch/r4_default_{1,2,3}.json gives 'status moved: none' - walk.power_max_hz FAILs in both groups (baseline 79.47 x3, candidate 80.09 / 79.47 / 79.47) and every deterministic check is equal (taste 10.93, smell 7.86 / 816, walk.GF_max 4.61, power_sustained 37.89, motion 0.23, bitter 5.52 / 139.90 / 0.82). My own seed-matched cluster pair confirms it: candidate out/sk_ntmaj_cand_{1,2}.json vs default out/sk_ntmaj_base_1.json, status moved none, walk.power_max 79.47 / 79.47 / 79.47, loom.GF_peak 50.38 x3. The report reached the same conclusion in prose ('would be met trivially against a fixed-benchmark default') but headlines the artefactual NOT MET and never used the available baseline.
* The W-identity argument does not by itself license the attribution in claim 4: the candidate and the r3 baseline differ in benchmark code state as well as in cache, and the baseline's header records model None, so 'the W identity attributes it to the benchmark fix' is an unsound inference step even though the conclusion is correct (see the confirmed entry for the evidence that does carry it).
* 'the type-majority rule covers 0 of the 27 rescued AL LN cells at any threshold' is true only for the proposed standard (no LN type has any independent source). The majority rule ALONE at (>= 1 labelled, share 1.0) would cover 8 of the 13 types / 16 of the 27 cells / 74,047 raw synapses, and would CONTRADICT the regex's GABA in 6 of those 8 types (11 cells, 13,534 syn: acetylcholine for lLN10 / v2LN3A / v2LN41, glutamate for v2LN40_2 / vLN26 / vLN29), agreeing on only lLN2F_a + lLN2T_d (5 cells, 60,513 syn). This strengthens the 'cannot replace the regex' conclusion; it should be stated with the numbers rather than as '0 at any threshold'.
* The example list of unknown-NT types with no labelled member ('INXXX045 31,122, AVLP538 27,352, PVLP106 21,957, DNd02 21,497, GLNO 17,698') is not the top five: PLP211 18,178 and SMP143 17,813 outrank GLNO, which is 7th (my independent ranking; the doc says 'e.g.', the report summary reads as a ranking).
* 'every room section is identical or within the baseline's own scatter' is not exact for rotate.DNp20_flip_hz: candidate -36.03 / -30.70 / -32.45 vs r3 baseline -22.02 / -31.72 / -29.60 lies partly outside the baseline range (it is inside the r4_default range -31.90..-34.04, and inside my own runs' -32.29 / -38.78 vs -34.19).
* MeTu1 has NO row in flyverse/data/nt_by_type_transcriptome.csv (I checked: 0 rows, no MeTu* rows at all); its only independent support is the Nern 2025 prediction. Under the literal round-4 item-7 wording ('the type majority and >= 1 transcriptome source agree') the proposal would be 2 types / 35 cells (JO-unclear, T2a); the implementation broadens the standard to 'transcriptome source OR Nern prediction', which the doc states but the report's claim 1 blurs. Outcome unchanged (0 synapses either way).
* The strength of the JO-unclear support (34 of the 36 proposed cells) is overstated by calling it 'FCA 2022 Johnston organ neuron cluster' without qualification: that row is tier 'class', confidence 'low', marker_rank 0.368, a pooled cluster (pool_labels acetylcholine:635) and has no Nern call (agreement 'transcriptome_model_agree_nern_unclear').
* 'docs/audits/nt_type_majority.md (491 lines, fully generated)': 491 lines is right, but section 6's decision paragraph is authored prose passed in via --note, not generated from data.
* 'job r4-ntmaj-b8f38e, 3 jobs, 0 failed, 11.1 min' is only partly checkable: the job name is corroborated by the receptor.table paths inside out/r4_ntmaj_*.json, and the per-run runtimes (7.9 / 7.7 / 6.2 min in the .txt files) make ~11 min plausible, but no job log was shipped to out/, so '0 failed' and '11.1 min' rest on nothing in the repository. My equivalent job (sk-ntmaj-439417, 4 jobs, all 'completed exit 0', log out/sk_ntmaj_cluster.log) is on record.
* Provenance note for future comparisons: the candidate cache out/cache_605ab2bd lacks sign0_counts.npz, which cache/ has. This is harmless here because connectome.receptor_signs only reads it with with_counts=True and brain._receptor defaults with_counts to False for 'sign' (the round-4 benchmark passes with_counts = (model == 'full')), so the candidate/baseline comparison is not confounded - but the report does not mention the missing file.
* Files I added (nothing of theirs was edited): scripts/skeptic_ntmaj_verify.py (independent re-derivation + JSON cross-tab), out/sk_ntmaj_rule_current.csv, out/sk_ntmaj_rule_pre.csv, out/sk_ntmaj_cluster.log, out/sk_ntmaj_build_{1,2}.txt, out/sk_ntmaj_cand_{1,2}.json/.txt, out/sk_ntmaj_base_1.json/.txt, out/sk_ntmaj_offchk.json/.txt, out/sk_ntmaj_majority.md.

### Confirmed

I wrote scripts/skeptic_ntmaj_verify.py from scratch (does not import audit_nt.py) against cache/ + the raw weight feather: proposed 3 types / 36 cells / 0 presynaptic / 0 raw output synapses / 0 edges = JO-unclear -> acetylcholine (34 unknown of 102, 68 labelled, share 1.00, mechanosensory), MeTu1 (1 of 250, 249 labelled, visual_projection), T2a (1 of 1872, 1871 labelled, optic). Support rows: JO-unclear fca2022 'Johnston organ neuron', pool_mixed False, pool_labels acetylcholine:635; T2a fca2022;kurmangaliyev2020;ozel2021 + Nern acetylcholine; MeTu1 type_map_nern2025.csv row 445 tier exact, nern_nt/validated_nt acetylcholine, validated_method 'EASI-FISH (Eddison et al)'. Every one of the 342 rows of the doc's section-2 table matches my table on module, n_cells, n_unknown, n_unknown_pre, unknown_out_syn, n_labelled, majority and majority_share (0 mismatches).
* CLAIM 2 (cache identity) REPRODUCED INDEPENDENTLY, locally and on the cluster. * CLAIM 3 (candidate suite runs) verified from the JSONs: out/r4_ntmaj_{1,2,3}.json are 26 PASS / 1 FAIL / 2 KNOWN GAP in 3/3 (29 checks), device NVIDIA B200, cache_dir out/cache_605ab2bd, receptor model sign / net_rule abs / flag default / fast_sign_changed_entries 48295, nt_counts unknown 2,325 and acetylcholine 104,080, runtimes 473.83 / 464.21 / 369.67 s; walk.power_max_hz 80.09 / 79.47 / 79.47 FAIL (criterion < 50); walk.GF_max 4.61 x3; power_sustained 39.91 / 37.89 / 37.89; loom.GF_peak 54.27 / 46.89 / 50.38; motion.min_dsi 0.23 x3; loom_escape.GF_peak 51.80 / 47.72 / 49.42 with 3 escapes x3; taste.MN9 10.93 x3; smell.KC_active 816 x3.

On cache/: 99 types / 443 cells / 402 presynaptic / 189,926 syn (glutamate 60,174, gaba 47,885, ACh 43,410, serotonin 11,919, OA 11,681, His 7,857, DA 7,000), 95 of the 99 types (406 cells, 188,608 syn) with no transcriptome call and no Nern prediction, unknown population 2,361 cells / 824,211 raw syn / 0.664 % of 124,161,873 / 1,732 presynaptic / 1,054 untyped (51,918 syn), 243 types with no labelled member (864 cells, 582,367 syn). The full threshold-sensitivity table matches row for row, including (1,0.5) 98/432/189,459, (4,0.8) 6/58/8,275 and (8,0.8) 3/36/0.
* CLAIM 6 (AL LN regex) REPRODUCED INDEPENDENTLY from the raw NT feather + connectome.UNKNOWN_NT_OVERRIDE_REGEX '^(lLN|v2LN|v3LN|il3LN|l2LN|vLN|LN)': 27 rescued cells in 13 types, 136,223 raw output synapses, largest v2LN30 51,308 / lLN2F_a 34,543 / lLN2T_d 25,970; 5 types with no raw-labelled member (l2LN22, v2LN30, v2LN31, v2LNX01, vLN27); labelled siblings gaba in 2 (lLN2F_a 2, lLN2T_d 1), acetylcholine in 3 (lLN10 1, v2LN3A 2, v2LN41 1), glutamate in 3 (v2LN40_2 1, vLN26 1, vLN29 1); no LN type has any transcriptome or Nern call, so 'proposed' is 0 at every threshold.
* CLAIM 7 (TmY14) CONFIRMED on out/cache_pre_override by my own count: 386 labelled cells = glutamate 209 + acetylcholine 177, share 0.5415, majority_rule False at share >= 0.8 -> the round-2 adoption did not rest on the sibling majority.
