# Dynamics round 2 (2026-09-13) -- skeptic verdicts

Four audit threads (build:transducer, run:takeoff-split, run:pm-bound, run:vnc-drive), each checked by an Opus skeptic; three verdicts **mostly sound** and one (vnc-drive) **unsound on its numbers** -- it had quoted a superseded staging directory -- with its conclusions confirmed at 5 runs per arm; the audit was rewritten from the final data afterwards.

## verify:transducer  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* CITATION ERROR (ledger, shipped file). Row lit.FeCO.afferent_rate_range_hz in D:\Projects\flyverse\flyverse\data\expected_responses.csv and section 2 of D:\Projects\flyverse\docs\audits\proprioception_transducer.md cite 'Matheson 1992, J Exp Biol 162:229'. No such paper exists. The paper whose content is described (range fractionation; tonic activity greatest at the joint extremes) is Matheson T (1992), 'Range fractionation in the locust metathoracic femoral chordotonal organ', J Comp Physiol A 170:509-520 (DOI 10.1007/BF00191466; Semantic Scholar returns exactly that venue/volume/pages). The other Matheson 1992 paper is also J Comp Physiol A (170:101-120), not JEB. This is the one citation of the fifteen I could not resolve as written; the Hz numbers attributed to it (tonic tens of Hz, movement units >100 Hz) are therefore unverified against any reachable source.
* SIDE-RULE COUNT IS STILL WRONG, in the very sentence the pass reports as its correction. KEY CLAIM 9 and the audit say 'agrees on 176 of the 180 decisive cells (|lat| > 0.2), disagrees on 4, weak 6'. That is internally impossible: 176 + 4 = 180 of 183 instance-labelled cells leaves 3 weak, not 6. Recomputed on the shipped cache with the class's own laterality(): 183 instance-labelled (chordotonal 22, hair_plate 1, campaniform 12, haltere 148); 6 weak (|lat| <= 0.2, all SNpp53); 177 decisive; 173 agree, 4 disagree. By the generator's own no-threshold counting (out/proprio/structure.txt) it is 177 agree / 6 disagree of 183. Neither reading gives 176/180. Everything else in that paragraph is exact (the 4 disagreements are all SNpp53; the 12 laterality values 0.08/0.39/0.58/0.23/0.03/0.06/0.34/0.37/-0.12/0.05/-0.02/0.69 against instances L R R L L R R L L L R R; 10 of 12 positive; the 171 sensory_ascending cells agree 171/171).
* '55 fly-runs' is wrong: it is 44. Every one of the 11 JSONs in D:\Projects\flyverse\out\proprio records batch 4, seconds 5.0, frames 500, so 11 x 4 = 44 fly-runs (220 fly-seconds). The substantive point survives unchanged: airborne_frac is 0.000 in all 11 runs, so the campaniform->0 and leg->tonic airborne branches are covered only by the CPU tests.
* The 250 Hz haltere ceiling is not in the source it is attributed to. The audit says 'the ceiling 250 Hz the wingbeat' and the ledger note says '250 Hz (one spike per stroke at the wingbeat frequency)', but the cited Lehmann & Dickinson 1997 (JEB 200:1133, verified) gives a Drosophila wingbeat of about 200 Hz and Fox & Daniel 2008 (JCPA 194:887, verified) gives afferent following up to 150 Hz. 250 Hz is above both; it is chosen headroom, not a literature number, and should be labelled as such (the row is op report, so nothing is scored on it).
* 'Bit-identical' is not demonstrable on the GPU at all, so the claim's scope must be stated as CPU. My cluster job (1 job, 0 failed, 10.3 min, B200, out/propbitid_cluster.log) ran the SAME working tree twice at the same seed with the sense off: work vs work2 differ (hops 7 vs 6, walking-GF median 31.247 vs 30.273, first brain-state hash difference at frame 500 of 6000). HEAD vs working tree differ by no more than that band (hops 5 / 7 / 6 / 4, GF median 29.914 / 31.247 / 30.273 / 28.182). The shipped GPU path (cuda graphs + kernels + event-driven + cuSPARSE) is run-to-run nondeterministic, so a GPU A/B says nothing either way. Full output: D:\Projects\flyverse\out\proprio_bitid\compare.txt.
* NEW DEFECT, opt-in path: a checkpoint round trip is not exact with the sense on. BatchSim.state_dict's names tuple does not include 'motor', so load_state_dict restores no motor snapshot and the transducer feeds a stale or zero MN rate for one frame after a resume. Likewise the new line 'if rows is None: self.motor = None' clears the snapshot only on a full reset, so a partial reset(rows=[...]) leaves the reset rows reading their pre-reset MN rates for one frame. Neither touches the shipped path (nothing is attached), and no test covers either.
* MINOR: the ledger row lit.campaniform_leg.afferent_rate_range_hz declares the population as entryNerve~^(ProLN|MesoLN|MetaLN)$, but the code's Proprioception.LEG_NERVES also admits ProAN, VProN, DProN and ProCN. Both resolve to the same 12 SNpp53 on this cache, so no number changes, but the ledger row and the selector are not the same rule.
* MINOR: the chordotonal commanded mean over the 5 'all' runs is 23.482 Hz (recomputed from the JSONs), reported as 23.4 in both the audit table and KEY CLAIM 5; it rounds to 23.5. The measured value 23.407 +- 0.446 is exact.

### Corrections
* docs/audits/proprioception_transducer.md section 1 and KEY CLAIM 9: replace 'agree on the sign on 176 of the 180 cells where the laterality is decisive (|lat| > 0.2) and disagree on 4' with 'agree on 173 of the 177 cells where the laterality is decisive (|lat| > 0.2) and disagree on 4'; the 183 instance-labelled cells split 177 decisive + 6 weak, all 6 weak being SNpp53. (If the generator's own no-threshold counting is meant instead, it is 177 agree / 6 disagree of 183 -- state which counting is used.)
* flyverse/data/expected_responses.csv, row lit.FeCO.afferent_rate_range_hz, and audit section 2: change 'Matheson 1992, J Exp Biol 162:229' to 'Matheson 1992, J Comp Physiol A 170:509-520 (range fractionation in the locust metathoracic femoral chordotonal organ)'.
* Audit section 5 and KEY CLAIM 10: '55 fly-runs' -> '44 fly-runs (11 jobs x 4 flies, 220 fly-seconds)'.
* Audit section 2 / the lit.haltere note: stop attributing 250 Hz to the wingbeat. The cited wingbeat is about 200 Hz (Lehmann & Dickinson 1997) and the measured following limit 150 Hz (Fox & Daniel 2008); say '250 Hz, chosen as headroom above the ~200 Hz wingbeat, not a measured ceiling'.
* Anywhere the audit says the shipped output is bit-identical, add 'on CPU'. On a B200 the shipped path is not reproducible run-to-run at a fixed seed (two identical runs of the same tree differ from frame 500; hops 7 vs 6), so a GPU A/B cannot establish or refute bit-identity for this model. Evidence: D:\Projects\flyverse\out\proprio_bitid\compare.txt, D:\Projects\flyverse\out\propbitid_cluster.log, generator D:\Projects\flyverse\scripts\skeptic_proprio_bitid.py.
* Add self.motor to BatchSim.state_dict's names tuple (and restore it in load_state_dict), and clear it for the selected rows on a partial reset, so a resumed or partially reset run with the sense on feeds the right MN snapshot; add a test. Alternatively document the one-frame discrepancy in section 7.
* Either widen the lit.campaniform_leg population to the seven nerves Proprioception.LEG_NERVES admits, or narrow LEG_NERVES to the three the ledger row names, so the row and the selector state the same rule.
* Section 5 and KEY CLAIM 5: chordotonal commanded is 23.48 Hz, not 23.4.
* Not required, but the open question the smoke leaves: the opt-in arm's effect on walk.power_max_hz (< 50, the anti_runaway.md bound) is unmeasured -- only the mean power (17.8 vs 20.7) was recorded. Worth one line in section 7 next to the other limitations.

## verify:takeoff-split  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* ESCAPE ROUTE: 'escape 39 vs 38 (1.026x [0.656, 1.605])' and 'escape 103.6 % of the excess' (KEY CLAIM 1) and G.6.4 item 3's 'Both routes and the GF tail attribute the same way ... unlike G.5 the histamine class carries the escape excess too (39 vs off 10, 3.90x [2.01, 8.09], p 3.9e-05)' do NOT replicate. My fresh-seed batch (16 job(s), 0 failed, 86.7 min, run dir sk-d2hold-39c9a1, D:\Projects\flyverse\out\sk_d2_hold_cluster.log; brain seeds 4/5/6, env 64-79/80-95/96-111, same protocol, same 13 code md5s, same three table md5s rebuilt in-job) gives holdOpticHis escape 19 vs shipped 30 = 0.633x [0.353, 1.116] p 0.152, and vs off 8 = 2.375x p 0.052 -- i.e. G.5's unattributable escape route, not the reported one. Pooling the two batches (7 runs/arm, 33,600 fly-s/arm, p_floor 0.00058): escape 58 vs shipped 68 = 0.853x [0.600, 1.210], the escape share of the excess is 80.0 % not 103.6 %, and BOTH 'off vs shipped' (z -1.92) and 'holdOpticGlu vs shipped' (z -1.88) come back compare-NULL on escape. 103.6 % was a one-batch high draw; the escape channel remains the weakly attributed one. (D:\Projects\flyverse\out\sk_d2_hold\sk_d2_hold_replicate.log)
* OFF'S ZERO VOLUNTARY RATE: 'off has 0 voluntary take-offs' is not a property of the arm. My off arm at brain seed 5 / environment seed 85 made a voluntary take-off (D:\Projects\flyverse\out\sk_d2_hold\off_5.json: hops 4 = 3 escape + 1 voluntary; that row's power_max 103.1 Hz, gf_max_walk 27.69 Hz -- the wing-power route, hops = escape + voluntary still holds). Record + replication: 18 room batches, 86,400 fly-s, 1 voluntary take-off. The audit's 'now 0 in 72,000 fly-s over 15 batches' was exactly true of the record when written (I enumerated all 15), but it breaks in the very next batch, so the statements that lean on the exact zero -- 'voluntary undetermined only because off's SD is 0', the Jeffreys 'infx [18.713, 98262.742]' and '0.000x [0.000, 0.044]' -- are batch-specific. At 7 runs voluntary against off is a plain compare RESULT (z +29.2, U 49, p 0.00058), not 'undetermined'.
* G.6.3 CLOSING PARAGRAPH, BOTH HALVES (docs/audits/receptor_integration.md). 'Every holdOpticGlu and every holdOpticRandom run lies inside off's range on every measure' is false: off's hop rates are [0.208, 0.833, 0.625, 0.417] per 1,000 fly-s, but holdOpticGlu's 4th run is 1.042 and holdOpticRandom's is 1.250; off's rows>=33 are [1, 3, 2, 2] but holdOpticGlu gives 4 and 5 and holdOpticRandom gives 4 twice. 'Every holdOpticHis run lies inside the shipped default's range on hops and GF' is false too: two His runs sit at 3.958 against shipped's minimum 4.167 (the same sentence then concedes this), and three of four His GF medians (30.17 and 31.53 below, 33.39 above) lie outside shipped's [32.31, 33.31]. The compare verdicts these sentences gloss are unaffected.
* TIER / QC OF THE HISTAMINE ROWS: KEY CLAIM 6's 'all 45 optic histamine rows are ... tier exact, QC pass' is wrong. Recomputed from flyverse/data/receptors_by_type.csv: tier is exact on 34/45, fuzzy on 9 (Dm3a, Dm3b, Dm3c, LC14a-1, LC14a-2, LC14b, Pm1, Pm2a, Pm2b) and alias on 2 (LPi34, l-LNv); qc is pass on 44/45 -- l-LNv is 'suboptimal_only'. Immaterial by weight (tier-exact rows carry 17,049 of 17,256 entries = 98.8 %, QC-pass rows 17,189 = 99.6 %; the 11 non-exact rows carry 207 entries, l-LNv 67), and the audit's own G.6.5 attaches the qualifier to the enumerated big types where it IS true, so this is a claim-text error rather than an audit error. Everything else in claim 6 holds: fast_sign_abs 0 and fast_net_abs 'none' on 45/45, fast_selection 'primary' on 45/45, flip_contested empty on 45/45, and no alt_sources entry on any of the 45 reports a non-'none' abs call.

### Corrections
* 'Dose is not the explanation' holds for ENTRIES and |W| but not for postsynaptic cells touched, and the audit does not name that. Recomputed from the cache: holdOpticHis perturbs 10,411 postsynaptic cells, holdOpticRandom 2,505, holdOpticGlu 2,091, the Brain-side arm 1,659 (the optic side's two classes have disjoint post-cell sets: 10,411 + 2,091 = 12,502). Cells-touched orders every arm monotonically with the outcome, while entries (27,207 null > 17,256 carries > 3,832 null) and |W| (87,920 null > 83,191 carries) do not. So claim 3's unqualified "'the larger perturbation carries it' is refuted" should read 'refuted when perturbation is measured in entries or |W|'; a breadth-in-cells account of the same five arms is not excluded. Worse, it cannot be excluded by this design -- an entry-matched subset of a class can never match that class's cell count (3,832 entries touch at most 3,832 cells) -- so the confound is structural, not just an un-run control. G.6.6 names the row-granularity limit but not this one.
* Additivity is ~82 %, not ~93 %, once the replication is folded in, and it is 82 % on all three routes rather than the reported batch's uneven 93 / 114 / 83 %. At 7 runs per arm: hops (118 + 0) / 144, escape (40 + 1) / 50, voluntary (78 - 1) / 94 -- a consistent 18 % shortfall. 'No cancellation and no synergy' survives; 'the two classes' excesses sum to the default's within the count scatter' is now better stated as 'sum to ~0.82 of it', and the pooled His/shipped hops ratio 0.840x [0.669, 1.054] makes a ~16 % remainder the point estimate rather than the reported 10 %. The reported batch's escape line over-summed (32 vs 28) in the opposite direction, which is the same scatter.
* 'GF median 26.80 = off's 26.80' (claim 2 and the report text) is equality to two decimals only: the pooled 64-row medians are holdOpticGlu 26.803645 and off 26.802207 Hz. The audit's own wording ('= off's 26.80 to the second decimal') is correct; the claim text drops the qualifier and reads as an identity.
* scripts/build_hold_tables.py's `--groups` default is `",".join(GROUPS)` and GROUPS now includes the three new ALONE groups, so a bare `python scripts/build_hold_tables.py` writes three extra tables AND loads the connectome cache (OpticRandom -> alone_table -> optic_row_entries -> connectome.load), which the script previously never needed. The shipped model defaults are untouched and out/ is git-ignored, but the script's default invocation and its dependency footprint did change; the new groups would sit better behind an explicit --groups opt-in.
* The claim text says the OpticRandom draw is 'a seeded uniform draw over the 69,818 row subsets of the OPTIC SIDE'; it is over the 33 entry-owning optic-side rows of the 48 (the CSV header and the builder docstring are precise about this). Immaterial -- the 15 entry-free rows cannot change a sum -- but the 48-vs-33 slip is worth fixing if the sentence is reused. Similarly, G.6.4 item 1's 'the CI on the glutamate class's share of the excess is bounded at roughly <= 0.23x / <= 0.044x' quotes ratios-to-the-default, not shares-of-the-excess (which are 3.5 % and 0.0 %); the word 'roughly' carries it but the two quantities are not the same.
* Cosmetic, and in another thread's file: out/d2_hold/d2_hold_result.json's provenance.flyverse_commit.modified_files begins 'ocs/INTERP.md' -- a one-character path truncation from flyverse/interp/common.py's provenance helper, not from this task's code. Also, 'brain.py md5 9caf67b2 = shipped gains of d1-hold-8ed117' is true but not evidenced by that run dir's own logs (they record no md5s); the equality is established instead by receptor_integration.md:1650/1974 plus the empty git diff on flyverse/brain.py, which is the stronger evidence and what should be cited.

## verify:pm-bound  --  verdict: **mostly sound**

### Refuted
* Nothing load-bearing was refuted. The two claims that do not survive as stated are quantitative/inferential, not structural: (i) the appeal to a '0.241-0.246 cross-batch band' to set aside the no_drive_clip min_dsi 'result' verdict is invalid, because 0.2371 falls below that band rather than inside it; (ii) the characterisation of the 1.52 Hz margin as '~2 excursions (0.63 / 0.72 Hz)' understates the scatter on record -- out/sk_les_holds_all/holdOptic_r{0..3}.json shows an 11.86 Hz spread within one of the very arms section 4 uses, and the report's own 'off' suite arm spans 1.56 Hz, already wider than the margin. Both are detailed under corrections; neither reverses the decision, and (ii) in fact argues harder for de-scoring.

### Corrections
* min_dsi band argument is self-contradictory -- the one substantive reasoning defect. KEY CLAIM 7 says no_drive_clip's motion.min_dsi 0.2371 is 'inside the default's 0.241-0.246 cross-batch band', and docs/audits/anti_runaway.md lines 914-916 makes the same move to set aside compare's 'result' verdict. 0.2371 is BELOW 0.241, not inside it. All six no_drive_clip draws (0.23701883-0.23713313) lie below every shipped-default draw on file: this batch 0.24109642-0.24109660, and the round-5 files 0.24109651-0.24595012. The cross-batch band therefore does not neutralise the verdict. Correct wording: 0.0040 below the lowest shipped-default draw on record, small against the 0.1 bound, and irrelevant to the decision because nothing is adopted.
* 'bit for bit' is overstated for two of the five quoted values. The three walk values ARE bit-identical at full float precision (48.48052978515625 / 20.109053071339925 / 4.629162311553955) -- that is the load-bearing part and it holds, in my 8 fresh draws too. But motion.min_dsi is not bit-identical anywhere: the six baseline draws take five distinct values (0.2410964184151673 .. 0.24109659755256557), and the round-5 files themselves split 0.24109651/0.24109660/0.24109660 against 0.24595012/0.24595003 in out/r5_adopt_default_1.json and _2.json. loom.GF_peak spans 43.5929-47.2241 in the batch. Those two are reproductions to 4 dp / within scatter, not bit-for-bit.
* The section's replicate scatter is materially understated, in the direction that favours the report's own conclusion. KEY CLAIM 8 and the doc put the scale at '~2 excursions (0.63 Hz round-4, 0.72 Hz here)'. But out/sk_les_holds_all/holdOptic_r{0,1,2,3}.json -- lesion.id 'holdOptic', kind 'hold_table', note 'scripts/build_hold_tables.py group Optic', i.e. the SAME arm section 4 uses -- give walk.power_max 64.9147 / 56.1737 / 68.0376 / 64.9147, an 11.86 Hz spread over 4 replicates. And the report's own 'off' suite arm spans 1.5598 Hz (95.54158-97.10139), already larger than the 1.5195 Hz margin it is defending. Caveat in fairness: that lesion set is a different harness (tool 'lesion', sections rest,taste,smell,walk,bitter, provenance.execution.backend shows cuda_kernels/cuda_graphs/event_driven false) so it is not a like-for-like replicate -- but its baseline (48.4805 x4), holdBrain (47.0012 x4) and off (95.5416 x3) agree bit-for-bit with the native suite, so the divergence is specific to holdOptic and not obviously a backend artefact. The honest statement is that the margin is inside the worst single-arm scatter on record, not two excursions wide.
* Section 4 presents holdOptic as five bit-identical draws (64.9147 x5) while the 4-replicate record of that same arm with 56.17-68.04 sits unmentioned on file. I tested the sensitivity: Spearman stays exactly -0.600 under every substitution (report value / pooled 9 draws / eager-mean 63.51 / worst draw 56.17), so the ordering finding -- the load-bearing one -- is robust. Pearson moves -0.787 -> -0.664 and p 0.213 -> 0.336. So quote the rank correlation, not the Pearson magnitude.
* spearman_voluntary -0.80 is a tie-breaking artefact. scripts/pm_bound_report.py line 133 ranks with np.argsort(np.argsort(x))+1, which has no tie correction, and the voluntary rates contain a tie (0.000 for both holdOptic and off). Tie-corrected (scipy) the value is -0.7379, and because of the tie |rho| = 1 is unreachable, so the quoted 0.083 floor does not apply to that row. The headline rows ('all' and 'escape') have no ties and are exact.
* The 'off' arm rests on 3 suite draws, below docs/INTERP.md 10.2's '>= 4 runs per arm' (the report's own table says '3 suite / 4 room'). It is also the only arm whose walk.power_max has scatter, and its 3-draw mean 96.3668 is what the Pearson consumes. It does not change the FAIL status (every draw 95.5-97.1, far above 50) or its rank, so (c)'s ordering is unaffected.
* The round-6 section runs docs/audits/anti_runaway.md lines 758-947 (to end of file), not the reported 758-937. The diff is +191/-0, append-only; nothing in rounds 1-5 was edited.
* Precision on the recommendation: switching line 85 to the "notnone" form does not remove the row from the tally. benchmark.py line 132 makes "notnone" pass iff the value is not None, so the check stays in the pass count as a row that can only fail when the measurement is missing -- exactly what loom.escape_cm does today (it prints 'PASS' in every table). 'De-scored, kept as report' is accurate in effect; 'unscored' is not literally true.

## verify:vnc-drive  --  verdict: **unsound**

The qualitative conclusions survive. The numbers do not: **125 of the 128 section-0/4/5 room numbers I could pin down match `out/vncd_stage/analysis/room_table.csv` -- the partial, superseded staging directory (A 5, B 4, C 3, D 4, E 4 runs) -- and only 3 match `out/vncd/analysis/room_table.csv` (5 runs per arm).** Section 6's compass table is from staged n = 1-2 runs. The one bench number quoted is from the CPU smoke and is wrong. The audit's own section 0 asserts "Every number below is from `out/vncd/analysis/`" and section 10 calls `out/vncd_stage/` "superseded" and says "no number above comes from" the smokes. Both statements are false.

Everything below is recomputed independently (scratchpad scripts) **and** cross-checked against the shipped `probe_vnc_drive.py analyse` output. My recompute reproduces `out/vncd/analysis/room_table.csv` with **0 mismatches on all 51 rows x 5 arms**, so the generator is sound; the document is what is wrong.

---

The audit's section 5 verdict cell "B +0.58, z +3.9, p 0.016 result; C +0.60 result; D -0.21 null; E +0.59 result" is the **staged** console line verbatim; the final line is `B:+0.468/z+3.2/p0.032 result C:+0.689/z+4.7/p0.008 result D:-0.215/z-1.5/p0.056 null E:+0.622/z+4.2/p0.008 result`.

32. "`30 job(s), 0 failed`" is not evidence of success. Every job line in `out/vncd/batch.sh` ends `> file 2>&1; tail -6 file`, so the job's exit status is **`tail`'s**, which is 0 whenever the file exists. A crashed `python` would have reported exit 0. ---

### Confirmed
**Arms (task item 2) -- fully confirmed.** Every one of the 25 room JSONs records the right spec, read from its own `stimulus.params` / `channel_names` / `haltere_coriolis` / `counts`: A `None`, no channels; B `'all'`, 4 channels, coriolis False; C `'chordotonal,hair_plate,campaniform'`, 3 channels; D `'haltere'`, 1 channel; E `'all+haltere_coriolis'`, 4 channels, coriolis **True**. All 25 on `cuda`; identical `LIFParams` (`receptor_model sign`, `receptor_net_rule abs`, `type_path_gain [["^(LC4|LPLC2)$","^DNp01$",3.0]]`, `w_syn 0.275`); one compiled-W md5 `ef23cc27bea13be7f6a96f3c04fd3737`; `mn_ref_hz 30.0`; identical cell counts; `flyverse_commit.commit = 'unknown'`, `dirty false`, `n_files 43` -- the "43-file source fingerprint" and HEAD `653179b` (section 9) are both correct.

**Section 2 structure -- fully confirmed** (paths files are byte-comparable between stage and final; structural, run-count-independent). 941 afferent cells (615 + 113 + 12 + 201 (yes)), `n_source_nodes = 41`; 183 instance-sided cells (22+1+12+148); raw input DNa02 **48,125**, PS196_b **6,032**, GLNO **9,371**; DNa02 direct 48 synapses, **+2.046 mV/post volley**, the only direct afferent input; PS196_b and GLNO direct **0**; `SApp -> PS059 -> b -318.1`; `SApp -> CB0675 -> PS196_b +637.2`; `SApp -> CB0675 -> GLNO +11.91`; `SApp -> CB0675 -> PS196_b -> GLNO +1.065e4`; `SApp -> GNG580 -> PS047_b -> PS196_b +2.699e4`; `SNpp45 -> IN13B001 -> AN04B003 -> b -3.015e4`; 702 = 386 + 316; PS196_b -> GLNO 1,801 synapses = **19.2 %** of 9,371; 28 of 126 links silent under nfB; `DNge115 -> PS047_b` on the top silent k = 3 walk.

**The three headline conclusions all survive re-derivation at 5 runs per arm.**
- **(1) The afferents reach the chain and stop at DNa02.** Every `result`/`null` the audit reports in the section-4 table holds at 5 v 5 except (7) and (8). AN04B003 0.589/0.314 -> **3.713/3.690**; PS196_b 0.193/0.519 -> **1.437/1.505**; GLNO 0.034/0.038 -> **0.664/0.657**; AN07B037_a stays 0.000-0.0035 in every arm; DNa02 L/R `null` under B; `IN19A003_L -2.27`, `IN12B014 -0.94/-0.89` `result`. Trace depth order and `DNa02 depth 6 null` hold (final +0.011 Hz, audit +0.012).
- **(2) Steering changes, not through DNa02.** Straightness 0.9956 -> 0.9818, z -20.7, **every B/C/E run (max 0.9893) below every A run (min 0.9948)** (yes). leg L-R +0.151 -> +0.300, z +33, `result`; |leg L-R| 0.331 -> 0.474, z +48. DNa02 L/R stay 0.015/0.084 -> 0.023/0.099. **Arm C alone reproduces the whole effect; arm D none of it.**
- **DNa02 decompose.** Both quoted nets round correctly on the final data too: **DNa02_L -303.4 -> -234.8** (audit -303 -> -235) and **DNa02_R -362.9 -> -342.3** (audit -363 -> -342); operating point +68 (L) / +21 (R) mV/s; in `g`, -1.52 -> -1.17 mV and -1.81 -> -1.71 mV against the 7.0 mV gap. `AN04B003/L -> DNa02_L +9.09 -> +57.35` (yes) (audit +9.1 -> +57.3), `AN04B003/R -> DNa02_R +4.75 -> +55.78` (audit +55.5), `PS059/L -90.78 -> -113.54` (audit -113.0), `PS059/R -49.44 -> -75.31` (audit -75.2), `SNpp45 -> L/R +39.97/+17.12`, `GNG580/L +0.10 -> +21.15`, and `AN04B003/L` vs `PS059/L` is the tool's own `cancelling_pair` under B/C/E. The lateralised-excitation and on-the-spot-cancellation reading is sound.
- **(3) The compass does not see the self-turn.** Bump `vs` 0.754-0.771, peak 250-255 Hz, drift within +-0.010 w/s in all 36 arm-phase-runs against 4.0 ideal; PS196_b's L-R moves the **same way in both turn directions** in the final data too (+3.52 ccw, +3.95 cw); GLNO L-R flat to 1.1 Hz; the yaw cells flip as they must. Conclusion intact.
- **(5) E as a positive loop through the haltere MNs -- confirmed, and the data are stronger than the audit says.** In the same phase with the same 20 Hz DNa02 pulse, the haltere MN rate is **A ~ 7.3 Hz, B ~ 9.7 Hz, E ~ 27.6 Hz** (per-run: E ccw 27.9/27.1/27.7), and the commanded haltere afferent rate is **B 9.38 -> E 80.10 Hz**. The only difference is the `(1 + 1.0.|yaw|)` multiplier on the afferents those MNs receive, so the loop is real and closed. Realised in-turn loop gain DeltaMN/Deltaafferent ~ **17.9/70.7 = 0.25**, above the 0.16 the audit computes for the ground operating point (section 8.3, which I confirm: D-A haltere MN +1.66 Hz per +10.20 Hz commanded = 0.163).
- **(3) Leg L-R is a fixed bias, not a lateralised signal -- confirmed, with one refinement.** In the room the leg L-R is **positive in all 16 flies of all 25 runs** (0/400 fly-runs negative), min per fly +0.097, max +0.385; it never flips. In the compass it *is* modulated with the correct sign by the turn (ccw raises it above both rests in 8/9 runs, cw lowers it below both in 9/9) but **never changes sign**: at 1.6 rad/s clockwise with DNa02 L-R = -18 Hz the leg L-R is still **+0.09 to +0.33 Hz**. So it is a fixed positive offset carrying a small turn-modulated component -- the audit's "fixed bias ... the legs follow it by tenths of a Hz" is right, and its "a larger L-R temporal SD" is right too (per-frame SD 0.42 (A/D) -> 0.52 (B/C/E)).
- **Transducer laws** in `senses.py` match section 1 exactly: chordotonal 10->150, hair plate 5->100, campaniform 50 (max 100), haltere k = 1 ceiling 250, `coriolis_gain_per_rad_s = 1.0`, `mn_ref_hz = 30`. The law check reproduces: `10 + 140.2.864/30 = 23.37` = the measured 23.376; `5 + 95.2.864/30 = 14.07` = 14.080. "haltere = the haltere-MN rate" is exact (10.7035 vs 10.7032). E's ground term `1 + 0.031 = 1.03` (yes).
- **(4) Opt-in -- confirmed.** `BatchSim(..., proprioception=None)` default; `FlyBrain.available_senses` adds `proprioception` only when a `Proprioception` is attached; `FlyBrain.proprioception()` calls `_require`; `BatchSim.step` guards on `"proprioception" in available`; `BatchBody.proprio_state` is a new method that nothing else calls; `state_dict`/`load_state_dict` only add the spec string to the config check. `flyverse/brain.py` and `flyverse/body.py`: `git diff` **empty**.
- The transducer-audit corrections the document carries forward in section 9 are quoted correctly (173/177, Matheson *J Comp Physiol A* 170:509-520, 44 fly-runs, 250 Hz headroom, CPU-only bit-identity, the `state_dict` motor omission).

---

### The >= 4-runs rule (task item 2, second half)

`docs/INTERP.md` 10.1(3): ">= 4 runs per arm, 5 when the effect is small -- three runs cannot give a `result`". `common.compare` only returns `underpowered` when `min(n) < 3` **or** `p_floor > 0.05`; at **3 v 5 the floor is 0.0357 <= 0.05**, so the tool *will* call a `result` on 3 runs. The staged analysis the audit quotes therefore called **29 `result` verdicts for arm C at n = 3 v 5**, in violation of the rule, and the audit flags **only one of them** ("C -0.101 `result` at n 3 v 5"). The other 28 are `yaw_sd_deg_s`, `yaw_sd_deg_s_median`, `yaw_cmd_sd_deg_s`, `straightness`, `DNa02_abs_LR_hz`, `DNa02_R_hz`, `leg_LR_hz`, `leg_abs_LR_hz`, `leg_L_hz`, `leg_R_hz`, `power_mean_hz`, `power_sustained_hz`, `haltere_mn_hz`, `AN04B003_{L,R}`, `AN07B035_{L,R}`, `AN06A026_{L,R}`, `PS196_b_{L,R}`, `LAL139_R`, `GLNO_{L,R}`, `IN12B014_{L,R}`, `IN19A003_L`. **Mitigation: all 29 survive at 5 v 5 with p 0.0079 in the final data** -- the substance is unaffected, the procedure was not followed and the document does not say so.

Arms B/D/E were quoted at 4 v 5 (floor 0.0159) -- at the rule's minimum. The compass numbers were quoted from **n = 1 (B, E) and n = 2 (A)** runs, far below the rule; the audit does not claim a `result` there, and correctly labels the flip statistics `underpowered`.

---

### Things the data show that the audit does not say (task item 6)

1. **Arm B lowers taste and olfactory responses by ~60 % in the bare-Brain bench** (`taste.MN9_hz` 10.93->4.15, `smell.PN_hz` 7.86->2.82, `smell.KC_active` 816->511) and nearly doubles `walk.GF_max_hz` (4.63->8.39). All still PASS loose bounds, but this is a broad cross-modal effect of 740 tonically-driven afferents on a bare `brain.Brain`, not just a `rest` redefinition problem. section 8(1)'s "every other check ... holds" understates the adoption cost.
2. **Under E, PS196_b reaches 11.0/7.5 Hz during the turn** (13x its rest rate) -- the stop-gap arm drives the efference-copy chain *hard*, which strengthens the audit's own point that the failure is the missing *sign*, not a missing *magnitude*. The audit never reports this.
3. **The E loop is stable, not divergent.** section 8(3) says "the E arm shows what a gain above 1 on that loop does". It does not: E converges to a 27.6 Hz haltere MN / 80 Hz afferent fixed point. The realised in-turn gain is ~ 0.25 (vs 0.16 on the ground) -- higher than the audit's quoted figure, and still well below 1.
4. **Two `result` rows have overlapping arm ranges** (`IN19A003_R` B, `power_max_hz` B), contradicting section 4's blanket no-overlap statement.
5. **Arm C flies did leave the table** (2 of 80 fly-runs) -- the effect is not confined to arm B as section 5's table implies.
6. **The batch's `0 failed` is structurally uninformative** because of the `; tail` exit-code shadowing in `batch.sh` (`out/vncd/batch.sh`). INTERP.md 10.1(3) asks the author to "read `'<n> job(s), 0 failed'`" -- here that read proves nothing.
7. The `BatchSim.reset` change only clears `self.motor` on a **full** reset (`if rows is None`), so the section 9.6 stale-MN-rate defect is *worse* for a partial reset than for a resume -- which section 9.6 states correctly, but section 8(3) then asks only for "the snapshot in `state_dict`", not the partial-reset path.
8. Arms B-E **cannot run without four uncommitted edits to `flyverse/` outside `interp/`** (`senses.py`, `fly.py`, `batch_sim.py`, `batch_body.py`), against INTERP.md 10.1(7) "Nothing in `flyverse/` outside `interp/` changes". section 0's "Nothing in `flyverse/` is edited by this task; every arm is the shipped default model" is true of authorship and misleading about dependency.

---

### Exact `RUNCOUNT_NOTE` text

```
Runs per arm (the replicate unit, docs/INTERP.md 10.1(3)): room 5 runs per arm for all five arms
(brain seeds 0-4, environment seeds 100s..100s+15, 16 flies x 60 s each; 25 room jobs, every one
`device cuda` -- 13 on a B200, 12 on an H200 -- none on CPU, none missing), at or above the >= 4-runs
rule for every arm, so every arm-vs-A comparison is 5 v 5 and its exact-U floor is p 0.0079 and a
`result` is reachable. Compass: 3 runs per arm (A / B / E, seeds 0-2) -- below the rule by
construction, so the flip statistics floor at p 0.10 (3 v 3) and every one is `underpowered`, while
the per-phase drift comparisons are 3 v 6 (floor p 0.024) and read `null`; no compass number is
quoted as a result. Bench: 2 draws per arm (A / B), but the rest / taste / smell / walk checks are
bit-identical between the two draws, so the bench carries one effective replicate per arm and is
read as pass / fail, never as a verdict. Every number below must therefore be the 5-run value from
`out/vncd/analysis/`; the 4-, 3-, 2- and 1-run values of the superseded staging directory
`out/vncd_stage/analysis/` (A 5, B 4, C 3, D 4, E 4 room runs; A 2, B 1, E 1 compass runs) are not
quoted anywhere in this document.
```

The last sentence is the one the document must be made true by; as it stands, it is false throughout sections 0, 4, 5 and 6.
